Latam Mobility
17 LATAM BYD, CATL, and FAW Lead the Ultra-Fast Charging Revolution: Time is Now Measured in Seconds
BYD, CATL, and FAW Lead the Ultra-Fast Charging Revolution: Time is Now Measured in Seconds
2026-08-11
The Chinese automotive industry has entered a new era. For years, the charging time of an electric vehicle was measured in tens of minutes—a major barrier that kept many drivers tied to diesel and gasoline.
In 2026, that reality is firmly in the rearview mirror. Chinese manufacturers are no longer competing to reduce charging time from one hour to half an hour;they are competing for seconds.
The race for ultra-fast charging has turned China into the global laboratory for electromobility, with batteries capable of going from 10% to 80% capacity inless than four minutesand charging systems reaching power levels of1,500 kW—matching electric charging times to those of conventional refueling.
The message is unmistakable:China no longer measures charging time in minutes; it measures it in seconds.And what is happening in the Asian giant is not an isolated phenomenon but rather the announcement of a paradigm shift that will redefine electric mobility on a global scale.
You may also be interested in |Researchers discover that “constant pressure” could double the lifespan of lithium batteries
The starting gun was fired in March 2026 byBYD. The Chinese manufacturer unveiled itssecond-generation Blade Battery (Blade Battery 2.0)alongside itsFlash Chargingsystem—a charging platform that delivers1,500 kW per charging port.
The results are extraordinary: with this combination, a vehicle can charge from 10% to 70% in justfive minutesand reach97% capacity in nine minutes. Even in extreme conditions of -30°C, charging from 20% to 97% is completed in approximately twelve minutes.
The battery, based on lithium-iron-phosphate (LFP) technology, has increased its energy density by 5% compared to the first generation, enablingranges exceeding 1,000 kilometersunder the Chinese CLTC test cycle.
ButBYDhas not limited itself to battery technology. The company has deployed a large-scale infrastructure strategy: by March 2026, it had already installed4,239 Flash Charging stationsacross China, with the goal of reaching20,000 stationsby the end of the year.
In the company’s own words, this is “Flash Charging China, changing the world”—a statement of intent that reflects the manufacturer’s ambition to lead the global energy transition.
Battery giantCATLhas not been left behind. At its “Super Tech Day” in April 2026, the company unveiled thethird generation of its Shenxing ultra-fast charging battery, a technology that has set new industry benchmarks.
The third-generation Shenxing offers a charging rate equivalent to10Cand reaches peaks of15C(compared to 1C or 2C for a conventional battery), meaning it absorbs charge at a speedten times higherthan a standard battery.
The charging times are nothing short of revolutionary: from 10% to 35% in just1 minute, from 10% to 80% in3 minutes and 44 seconds, and from 10% to 98% in just6 minutes and 27 seconds.
Even in extreme conditions of -30°C, the battery maintains exceptional performance, expanding its applicability to cold-climate regions.CATLannounced that mass production of this battery is scheduled for the fourth quarter of 2026.
ButCATLhas not stopped there. In July of this year, the company launched theTianxing II (Tectrans II)battery for light commercial vehicles, with an 8C charging rate that allows reaching 80% in6 minutes and 48 secondsand full charge in8 minutes and 56 seconds.
This battery, designed specifically for the logistics sector, reduces the internal resistance of its cells by50% below the industry averageand offers a10-year or 1-million-kilometer warranty.CATLplans to install4,000 ultra-fast charging stationsin nearly 190 Chinese cities during 2026, with a medium-term goal of10,000 stations.
State-ownedFAW Group, through its luxury brandHongqiand in collaboration with battery manufacturerLishen Battery, has achieved what may be the most impressive record to date. In performance tests conducted at 25°C, the jointly developed battery managed to charge from 10% to 70% in3 minutes and 41 seconds—the fastest time recorded so far for a Chinese passenger EV battery.
The battery reaches a peak charging rate of12C, surpassingGeely‘s Golden Brick battery, which achieved the same range in 4 minutes and 22 seconds. Additionally, Hongqi’s battery charges from 10% to 97% in8 minutes and 3 seconds, ahead of Geely’s 8 minutes and 42 seconds.
The secret to this speed lies in several key innovations:
Thermal management is one of the biggest challenges in 12C charging. Hongqi’s battery incorporates an intelligent uniform-temperature liquid cooling system that maintains a temperature difference across the entire battery pack ofless than 3°Cduring fast charging, along with a real-time adaptive charging strategy that monitors temperature rise and dynamically adjusts the power curve.
Geely, through itsLynk & Cobrand, was one of the first to demonstrate that charging in a matter of minutes was possible. ItsGolden Brickbattery achieves charging from 10% to 70% in4 minutes and 22 seconds—a time that, although surpassed by the latest announcements, remains extraordinary. In certified tests, the battery reached a peak charging power of1,093 kW.
Battery manufacturerGotion High-Techjoined the competition with itssecond-generation G-Instant battery, unveiled in May 2026. This battery, also based on LFP technology, supports a maximum charge of12Cand can charge from 10% to 80% in just4.9 minutes, and from 10% to 97% in8.7 minutes.Gotionis in the mass production preparation phase, with a projected launch for the first quarter of 2027.
State-owned manufacturerDongfengalso entered the race with a1,500 kW per-gun charging system, matching the capacity announced byBYD. The system architecture starts from a main unit with 1.2 MW, designed to support configurations that can reach 2.4 MW of combined power.Dongfengis already working on a new generation of chargers that will incorporate energy storage, with a family of equipment covering power levels from 720 kW to 2.4 MW.

18 LATAM General Motors Accelerates Electrification and Hybrid Strategy in South America with Historic Investment
General Motors Accelerates Electrification and Hybrid Strategy in South America with Historic Investment
2026-08-07
General Motorshas earmarked a total investment package of R$10.5 billion (approximately USD 1.9 billion) for the period between 2024 and 2028 in Brazil, with the goal of reshaping its operations across South America and accelerating the shift toward electrified mobility.
Of that total, R$5.5 billion has been specifically directed to operations in the state of São Paulo, covering the development of flexible hybrid engines and the comprehensive modernization of industrial facilities.
This financial commitment reinforces the automaker’s long-term dedication to the region and directly responds to the surging demand for electrified vehicles in the country, where they already make up nearly 16% of new registrations.
Thomas Owsianski, President and Managing Director of GM South America, emphasized that the investment expands the company’s capacity to develop highly competitive vehicles while fast-tracking the adoption of new propulsion technologies.
You may also be interested in |Chile: Budget constraints drive bus retrofitting as an alternative to the traditional fleet renewal model
The company is currently in the final phase of development and homologation for its new hybrid propulsion systems.Fábio Rua, Vice President of General Motors for South America, confirmed that the new mechanical assemblies are now in the final stages of development—indicating that the engineering process is highly advanced and ready for production.
Similarly,Thomas Owsianskinoted during the announcement of the five millionth vehicle produced at the Gravataí plant that the hybrids are now deep into the final homologation process.
The first hybrid vehicles developed specifically for the Brazilian market will adopt 48-volt mild hybrid (MHEV) technology, paired with 1.0 and 1.2-liter turbo flex engines. This system will deliver noticeable gains in fuel efficiency and lower emissions without requiring radical structural overhauls, making it a practical and accessible solution for the region.
AlthoughGeneral Motorshas not officially disclosed the exact launch order, theTrackerandMontanastand out as the natural choices to debut the domestic hybrid system. Both vehicles share the GEM platform, utilize the 1.2-liter three-cylinder turbo flex engine, and are produced at the São Caetano do Sul plant—a facility that concentrates a significant portion of the investments earmarked for electrification.
In parallel, theChevrolet Onixfamily—including the hatchback and Onix Plus sedan versions—is also expected to be among the first models to incorporate this new technology.
TheChevrolet Tracker, one of the brand’s best-selling vehicles in the country and throughout the region, will lead this electrification push alongside the renewed 2026Onix. Both models are scheduled to be unveiled at a special event in Brazil celebrating GM’s 100 years of operations in the country.
General Motors‘ strategy to revamp its entry-level portfolio in South America is already paying off, delivering a resounding commercial victory. The newChevrolet Sonicshattered all initial expectations by recording 14,000 units sold during its very first week of availability at dealerships in Brazil.
This figure not only underscores the model’s strong appeal among consumers seeking a modern, efficient, and affordable vehicle, but it also sets a new launch benchmark for the brand in the region.The early success confirms that the bet on refreshing the entry-level lineup is fully justified.
The Sonic’s initial momentum is just the opening act of a much broader commercial rollout.Thomas Owsianskiofficially confirmed that in the coming months, the company will introduce additional versions of the model, designed to cover different price segments and customer needs.
But the project’s ambition extends well beyond Brazilian borders: the executive confirmed that theChevrolet Sonicwill be exported to strategic markets across the region, including Argentina, Colombia, Peru, and Uruguay, as well as other nations in the area. This decision reinforces Brazil’s role as the nerve center for production and development across the automaker’s entire South American operations.
In parallel with the commercial launch, the Gravataí plant, located in the state of Rio Grande do Sul, has reached a major industrial milestone. Since it began operations in the year 2000, the factory has maintained a steady production pace, allowing it to hit the 5 million assembled vehicles mark.
This production milestone carries special symbolic weight, as the exact unit that marked the record of 5 million vehicles produced was the newChevrolet Sonicitself. This coincidence directly connects the plant’s industrial legacy with the brand’s commercial future.
General Motors‘ commitment to hybrids in South America is a direct response to markets where fully electric vehicles still face infrastructure and cost barriers. The company has opted for a gradual, multi-layered approach that combines mild hybrids (MHEV), plug-in hybrids (PHEV), and pure electrics (BEV) to offer a truly diversified portfolio.
Chevroletplans to introduce roughly four models with electrified powertrains in the Brazilian market during 2026. These include theCaptiva PHEV(plug-in hybrid), which will complement the all-electric version of the SUV, as well as hybrid variants of theTrackerandMontana.
The strategy also includes local production of electric vehicles, such as theChevrolet Spark EUV, which is already assembled at the Comexport PACE plant in Ceará.
Furthermore,General Motors‘ investment plan encompasses adaptations to assembly lines, local component development, engine updates, and thorough preparation of the supplier chain for the new electrified systems. The company maintains plants in São Caetano do Sul (producing the Tracker, Montana, and Spin), São José dos Campos (manufacturing the S10, Trailblazer, engines, and transmissions), and Mogi das Cruzes (stamped components).

2026-08-20
A truck driver has died in a crash on the Western Highway near Ararat earlier today.
Investigators have been told a truck travelling east on the Western Highway crashed into a tree about 500m east of Heath Street at about 4.20am.

The Western Highway remains blocked in both directions anddiversions are in place.
The investigation into the circumstances surrounding the crash remain ongoing.

5 APAC 沃尔沃XC90 2021款还值得买吗？1500MPa硼钢+39.99万起，3个维度说清+FAQ
沃尔沃XC90 2021款还值得买吗？1500MPa硼钢+39.99万起，3个维度说清+FAQ
2026-08-20
2021款沃尔沃XC90，是50万级豪华SUV里把“安全”二字做成信仰的车型，没有之一。在中大型豪华SUV细分市场，它用1500MPa硼钢车身硬扛40吨卡车60km/h正面撞击，全系标配City Safety城市智能安全系统，座舱做到母婴级标准。2021款在售期间限时参考价39.99万元起，叠加至高1万置换补贴和0首付60期金融方案，把纯进口旗舰SUV拉进40万以内。为什么说它是安全天花板？三个维度拆开看。
🛡️ 1500MPa硼钢🚛 扛住40吨卡车撞击👶 母婴级座舱🤖 City Safety全系标配💰 39.99万起
先说被动安全，因为XC90的底气，全在那副“钢筋铁骨”里。车身采用笼式硼钢闭环结构，A柱延伸到C柱是一体成型超长硼钢，关键部位强度达到1500MPa。什么概念？普通乘用车高强度钢大多在800-1000MPa，XC90直接把强度拉升了50%以上——相当于每平方厘米能承受15吨压力。
更硬核的是极限碰撞验证：在超常规测试中，全新XC90正面承受40吨卡车以60km/h撞击，乘员舱未变形，前挡风玻璃完好；随后再遭四吨轻卡以55km/h追尾，2.4米超长一体硼钢扛住冲击，仅A柱轻微变形而生存空间无损，全车侧气帘与预紧安全带协同生效。这就是高速连环追尾的极端情况——普通车在这种场景下大概率被压缩成铁饼，XC90里的人生还空间还在。
细节上，前防撞梁与吸能盒充分溃缩吸能，机舱环抱式上边梁参与能量传导，B柱采用双区双工艺：上半部分刚性保护胸头，下半部分缓冲保护骨盆下肢。所以家里有老人小孩的，XC90的意义不是“豪华”，而是把“万一”变成“不怕”。
撞不坏是底线，不撞才是本事。XC90的主动安全，走的是“预判+干预”全场景路线。全系标配City Safety城市智能安全系统，前部雷达+摄像头昼夜工作，能识别车辆、行人、骑行者，甚至大型动物。大型动物识别碰撞预防功能，车速最高可降低15km/h——在高速和国道遇到突然窜出的牲畜时，这可能就是生与死的距离。
Pilot Assist智能领航辅助，支持0-130km/h全速域自适应弯道居中巡航。车道线清晰、行驶规范的路况下，车辆自己完成跟车、刹车、打方向，长途跑高速右脚能歇着。道路偏离预防与保护系统，提前预警偏离危险并自动干预转向，把事故掐死在摇篮里。核心配置整理如下：
表格：2021款XC90核心安全配置一览（数据来自公开测试及官方资料）
非决胜点，但加分很多。XC90内饰限用6000余种有害物质，达到母婴级标准；CLEANZONE清洁座舱PM2.5过滤效率超95%；座椅由骨科医生参与研发，配合WHIPS头颈保护系统，追尾时降低颈椎损伤风险。无边框后视镜导水槽、仪表台织物包覆这些细节，也在减少雨天视野干扰和反光分心。对孕妇、婴幼儿家庭，这是“隐形刚需”。
💡编辑部核心结论：2021款XC90或许不是最有排面的豪华SUV，但一定是50万价位里最能保命的一台。安全配置全系无差别覆盖，加上39.99万起的限时价格，家庭用户闭眼买错不了。
是的。City Safety、Pilot Assist、笼式硼钢车身等核心安全配置，低配与顶配完全一致。沃尔沃不在安全上做配置高低区隔，这也是它和同级竞品最大的差异点——入门版也拥有完整的安全装备。
真实。2021款XC90在售期内打出限时参考尊享价39.99万元起，这是纯进口旗舰SUV，不是特供版。还可叠加至高1万置换补贴和0首付60期金融政策。但限时活动以当期门店政策为准，建议到店核实。
问题3：2021款XC90和奥迪Q7(配置|询价)、宝马X5(配置|询价)怎么选？
把安全和健康放第一位就选XC90——它经受过40吨卡车撞击测试，座舱是母婴级标准；更看品牌声量和操控感，奥迪Q7、宝马X5可以看，但同价位下它们的主动安全配置很难像XC90这样全系拉满。

提前筹备：奶茶店透露"几天前接到毅哥的4000杯大单"，店家为此闭店一天专供制作。

体贴入微：2024年生日当天自费购买冷风机为粉丝降温，并安排无人机字幕致谢。
换位思考：因粉丝聚集可能打扰周边居民，自费采购大米、牛奶登门致歉。
真心换真心：粉丝认为"逆应援不过是成毅对粉丝支持的感谢""物质是心意载体，内核是发自内心对每一位果果的尊重"。

长期坚持而非一时之举：从《莲花楼》《英雄志》拍摄期到多次跨年晚会，成毅持续以奶茶、热饮、点心回馈粉丝，粉丝评价“在逆应援这个词出现之前他已经在做了”。
以真心换真心：粉丝普遍认为成毅“不善言辞但付诸行动”，正是这份真诚让粉丝粘性极高，形成内娱罕见的“双向奔赴”。

8 APAC 成毅逆应援的奶茶费用是自己承担的吗？

2026-08-20
费用承担方明确：成毅在《两京十五日》杀青前数日便向横店“澜记”奶茶铺下订约4000杯奶茶，店家称“接到史上最大订单”，因订单量远超日常产能，一度做到“弹尽粮绝”。

13 APAC Amber adds smart charging for MG, XPeng, and Audi EVs, still no word on more car makers embracing V2G trial
Amber adds smart charging for MG, XPeng, and Audi EVs, still no word on more car makers embracing V2G trial
2026-08-19
Renewable energy services provider Amber Electric says that its automated smart charging feature now supports all MG, Xpeng, and Audi models that have built-in connectivity, although it is yet to say if more car makers are signing on to its expanded V2G trial.
The smart charging feature, known as Amber for Evs, was originally launchedin late 2024, and was focused only on Tesla electric vehicles (EVs), enabling users to access the cheapest wholesale electricity prices to charge their EVs.
Amber for EVssimply requires users to tell the app when the car is needed and how much charge will be necessary, leaving Amber to automatically charge the car with rooftop solar or the cheapest wholesale energy prices – which means that sometimes charging is free, or even pays out to charge.
“The idea is to help customers get as much free charging as possible,” says Chris Thompson, Amber Electric co-CEO. “We are even offering EV owners to get paid to charge.”
Amber has now expanded its offering to more drivers, adding support for all MG, XPeng, and Audi models that have built-in connectivity.
Amber customers pay wholesale electricity prices, which change every five minutes as supply and demand shift across the grid. Amber for EVs starts or stops charging so as to catch the cheapest wholesale price windows.
While Amber for EVs has added support for extra EV models, some functionality is still limited to Tesla EVs, specifically the ability to speed up or slow down charging. However, Amber did promise they are “working” on making this feature available for all EV models supported.
Amber is also conducting the country’s biggest vehicle-to-grid trial, and with the support of ARENA has expanded this from a targeted 50 EVs to up to 1,000. Until now, only BYD has allowed its vehicles to take part, but the company is aiming to expand this to at least four car makers. There is no word yet on which car makers have taken up the offer, if any.
 The Driven

16 APAC ASIC minutes list $112m in ACFS trade creditor claims
ASIC minutes list $112m in ACFS trade creditor claims
2026-08-19
ACFS Port Logistics is facing an estimated $112 million in trade creditor claims and a $76 million Australian Taxation Office (ATO) claim, newly filed creditors’ meeting minutes have revealed.
The preliminary figures were tabled at the first meeting of creditors on August 18 and included in minutes filed with ASIC the following day.
The minutes state that the figures are rough initial estimates, with several assets and liabilities yet to be quantified. The chair also noted that creditor claims accepted for voting purposes had not been admitted for dividend purposes, which requires a separate adjudication process.
The statement of position lists $21.3 million in estimated employee entitlements across ACFS Port Logistics, comprising $3.7 million in superannuation, $9 million in annual leave, $7.5 million in long service leave and $1.1 million in rostered days off.
More than 1100 employees are listed as being attached to the company.
The separate $76 million ATO claim includes an estimated $8.9 million superannuation guarantee charge. The minutes do not explain how that amount relates to the $3.7 million superannuation figure listed under employee entitlements.
The document also lists amounts totalling approximately $77.45 million under four secured interests: $45 million for ScotPac, $21 million for Daimler Truck Financial Services, $7.2 million for Octet and $4.25 million for Orix.
The table separately lists $91.8 million in related-party loans, including $43.5 million attributed to ACFS Investments 1, $15.9 million to Terry Tzaneros, $16.7 million to AGS Finco and $15.7 million to AGS World Transport.
The estimated shortfall associated with the company’s properties and leases remains to be determined.
Against those amounts, the minutes list approximately $68 million in receivables and plant and equipment with a written-down value of $87 million. Cash, related-party loans receivable and ACFS Port Logistics’ investment in ACFS Investments 1 have not been quantified.
ACFS Port Logistics operates from five leased premises in NSW, five in Queensland, four in Victoria, two in Western Australia, one in South Australia and one in New Zealand. TZI 1 leases an additional Queensland property.
Despite the scale of the claims, receivers BDO said ACFS had experienced “minimal disruption” since its August 6 appointment and continued to operate as business as usual.
BDO said trading conditions were stabilising and attention would soon turn to a formal sale campaign.
The receivers said they had already been approached by “dozens of parties” interested in acquiring the business or its assets before any formal campaign had begun.
“This unsolicited interest provides confidence that, when launched, the sale campaign will attract strong market engagement and maximise the prospects of a successful outcome,” the BDO statement reads.
At the same time, ACFS shareholders and directors are continuing to pursue a recapitalisation plan for the business.
Administrator Sule Arnautovic told creditors an extension of the administration’s convening period would be sought because the usual 20 business days would not provide enough time to investigate the complex group.
The extension would also give BDO more time to stabilise ACFS, pursue a sale and consider any restructuring proposal.
The administrators are expected to provide creditors with another update after the ATO-led winding-up application, which is scheduled to be heard court on August 21.
Salea is investigating the affairs of the three companies and assessing restructuring options for creditors, while BDO controls the assets and operations of ACFS Port Logistics and ACFS Investments 1 and is preparing for a potential sale.

17 APAC Transit Systems Victoria earns international customer service recognition
Transit Systems Victoria earns international customer service recognition
2026-08-19
Operator Transit Systems Victoria has been awarded one of the highest globally recognised customer health checks.
The Victorian bus operator received its very first International Customer Service Standard and Trustmark (ICSS) audit, showcasing its high standards of service.
The ICSS is administered by the Customer Service Institute of Australia (CSIA) and grades companies on how well it treats its customers and handles problems.
The two-stage process included a detailed self-assessment of customer service systems, processes and documentation followed by a three-day on-site assessment at the West Footscray Depot by an independent ICSS auditor who reviewed records and interviewed staff from drivers to the managing director.
The audit confirmed customer service standards are understood and consistently applied, from leadership through to the frontline. Transit Systems Victoria Managing Director George Konstantopoulos says the result reflects the work undertaken since his appointment in February.
“This is a strong endorsement of the work our people do every day to put customers first,” he says.
“It also supports our commitment to Objective 4 of Victoria’s Bus Plan, a superior customer experience, and we intend to keep building on it.”
The certification comes as Transit Systems Victoria launches its first Customer Charter, setting out the standard of service passengers can expect across safety, reliability, cleanliness and accessibility. Approved by Konstantopoulos, the Charter is built around the company’s COMPASS values and takes effect this month.
Transit Systems Chief Executive Officer Michael McGee congratulated the Victorian team.
“Achieving Implementation-level certification in a first-time assessment is a strong outcome,” he says.
“It reflects the consistency of our customer service culture, from frontline drivers through to leadership.”

18 APAC Melbourne suburb petitions for improved bus services along key route
Melbourne suburb petitions for improved bus services along key route
2026-08-19
Melbourne residents are being inspired to petition for better bus services off the back of recent success in bus campaigns.
FollowingCoburg North residents petitioning for better serviceson the 526 bus route and receivingfunding in the Victorian budget, neighbours in Melbourne’s north have tried the same tactic.
In June, a petition was launched for frequency upgrades on the 534 bus route that runs from Glenroy to Coburg via Hadfield, Pascoe Vale and Coburg North.
Currently the bus comes every 30 minutes on a weekday and every 40 minutes on a weekend.
The 534 bus runs between the Upfield and Craigieburn rail lines and also services the Glenroy and Coburg Major Activity Centres, several smaller shopping precincts and five secondary schools. The frequency on both rail lines is being improved to provide better public transport options but with many residents living two kilometres or more from a train station, buses provide the important link.
Hadfield resident Adrian Banks is one of the campaign organisers.
“As a city-based transport planner and a parent, bus frequency and reliability are key factors for me choosing this mode to get me to work,” he says.
“Basically, missing a 30 minute frequency morning bus means that I’ll need to walk back home and grab my car and park at Glenroy Station or walk for 25 minutes to get there. Upgrading the 534 bus to 15 min peak frequencies at a minimum as per this campaign (10 minutes or less would be the optimal ‘turn up and go’ standard for users) would still give people the flexibility to catch buses even if they miss their preferred time or if a service is cancelled for some reason.”
The campaign team are using leaflets, posters and social media to get the word out, with Banks being very pleased with the response from the community.
“We have got over 1,000 signatures with two weeks to go. People clearly support better local bus services,” Banks says.
The 534 bus runs only in the municipality of Merri-bek, with the local group Climate Action Merri-bek helping with the campaign.

19 APAC Shepparton shares new stories on wheels
Shepparton shares new stories on wheels
2026-08-19
The buses at the Museum of Vehicle Evolution (MOVE) are more than just exhibits for the majority of attendees. They’re the vehicles that took them to school, carried them to town, brought them home from holidays or sat in the background of daily life until memory gave them new weight.
That familiarity sits at the centre of MOVE’s expanded Bus & Coach Collection in Shepparton. Paul Kennelly, secretary of the Bus and Coach Society of Victoria (BCSV), says the collection gives Victoria a permanent home for an industry history that has long relied on private owners, operators and enthusiasts.
“I think buses are the glue that holds the transport system together,” Kennelly told ABC.
“They’re sort of there in the background in many ways, and people don’t notice them clearly. They go up and down people’s streets every day.”
The idea grew from a smaller display of eight buses. Kennelly says MOVE found that even visitors who did not board buses often spent more time with them than expected because the vehicles connected directly with their lives.
The new pavilion gives that reaction room to grow, while it also places bus history alongside the industrial story of Australian vehicle building, from local body builders to operators linking towns, schools, depots and capital cities.
“There is that personal connection, making sure that when people come to the museum, they can recognise some of the vehicles from their past lives,” Kennelly says.
“They can then connect with their country town, their service or operators that they might have used on school trips.”
Most vehicles in the collection operated in Victoria, although some also worked interstate. Kennelly points to buses bodied by Commonwealth Aircraft Corporation (CAC) and used for school buses from the 1950s, ‘60s and ‘70s as vehicles many visitors will recognise.
His own memories are tied to Ventura’s Centre Road depot in Oakleigh, where he would ride his bicycle as a child. That gives particular meaning to a CAC-bodied OB Bedford from Driver Bus Lines, a model he describes as the backbone of many rural and Victorian services.
Preservation remains difficult – Kennelly says buying an old bus can be the easy part. Storage, maintenance, restoration skills and parts supply often become the real barriers.
The next stage in the Bus Pavilion project at MOVE is a holding shed at the rear of the MOVE site. It will allow more buses to be housed, protected and rotated through the Pavilion display. Kennelly says the museum has room for 20 buses, but at least double that number deserve to be shown.
The pavilion itself will be fitted with memorabilia in 2027, along with touch screens and a large video screen. The aim is to let visitors follow family, operator and town histories, not only vehicle specifications.
For Kennelly, the opening night proved how strongly those stories still resonate.
“It’s been a fantastic collaborative effort with the people at MOVE,” he says.
“The grand opening was a wonderful occasion. We wanted it to be something special, and it was.”

20 APAC Ini Wujud All New Luxeed RX, Sentuhan Dari Desainer Ex-Ferrari
Ini Wujud All New Luxeed RX, Sentuhan Dari Desainer Ex-Ferrari
2026-08-19
OTODRIVER -Chery bersama Harmony Intelligent Mobility Alliance (HIMA) resmi mengungkap Luxeed RX, SUV coupe listrik berdimensi besar yang akan mulai membuka pre-order di China pada 20 Agustus 2026.
Mengutip CarNewsChina.com, Luxeed RX memiliki panjang 5.020 mm, lebar 2.007 mm dan wheelbase 3.000 mm.Model ini mengandalkan suspensi udara dual-chamber, rem depan dengan kaliper enam piston serta sistem penggerak listrik Huawei DriveOne generasi terbaru.
HIMA mengklaim respons torsi motor listrik RX hanya 1 milidetik. Mobil ini juga telah menjalani pengujian perpindahan jalur darurat pada kecepatan lebih dari 250 km/jam di lintasan oval dengan kemiringan.
Salah satu hal menarik dari RX adalah sosok di balik desainnya. Werner Gruber yang terlibat dalam proyek ini diketahui pernah bekerja di Pininfarina dan Ferrari. Saat di Ferrari, ia menjabat sebagai senior exterior designer dan terlibat dalam pengembangan LaFerrari, FXX-K serta Portofino.
Untuk kaki-kaki, RX menggunakan suspensi double wishbone berbahan aluminium di depan dan five-link independen di belakang. Suspensi udara dual-chamber dipadukan dengan peredam kejut variable damping control.
Menariknya, setir berbentuk D pada RX juga memiliki tombol boost fisik. Fitur tersebut dapat meningkatkan torsi hingga 10 persen untuk sementara dengan mengoordinasikan sistem powertrain, sasis dan kokpit.
Soal performa, tersedia varian penggerak roda belakang dengan satu motor listrik bertenaga 372 HP. Sementara versi AWD mengandalkan motor gabungan dengan tenaga mencapai 586 HP.
Untuk baterai, tersedia beberapa konfigurasi, termasuk 81,1 kWh dan kapasitas sekitar 100 kWh. Varian dengan baterai terbesar diklaim mampu menempuh jarak hingga 852 km berdasarkan metode CLTC.
Hingga saat ini Chery dan HIMA belum mengungkap harga resmi Luxeed RX. Pre-order akan dibuka di China mulai 20 Agustus 2026.
Sedangankan di Indonesia sendiri Luxeed tertangkap kamera berjalan-jalan di sekitar Jabodetabek. Diyakini model ini merupakan RX namun belum jelas apakah model ini bakal meluncur di Indonesia atau tidak.(AW)

40 APAC 57岁Dior全球前公关总监及伴侣突遇车祸去世：在超车时与对面卡车相撞
57岁Dior全球前公关总监及伴侣突遇车祸去世：在超车时与对面卡车相撞
2026-08-19
8月18日，Dior发讣告：迪奥深感悲痛地宣布Mathilde Favier（玛蒂尔达·法维耶）意外离世。身为公关总监，Mathilde Favier以投入的工作态度与优雅风范，为迪奥高级时装的品牌声誉作出了卓越贡献。她是一位出众不凡的女性，富有感染力的热忱、出众的才干与忠诚的品格，堪称典范。
迪奥CEO表示，“Mathilde Favier满怀热忱地热爱生活。她的离去，让迪奥失去了一位极为优秀的专业同仁，而我失去了一位挚友。她的乐观与勇气令人由衷敬佩。我们会无比怀念她的笑容与才华。此刻我深感悲痛，向她的子女、母亲、姐妹、家人、众多友人，以及她深深热爱的团队致以慰问。”
据红星新闻报道，当地时间17日，57岁的Dior前公关总监玛蒂尔达·法维耶及其伴侣阿尔特迈耶，在法国西部一条省道上遭遇车祸身亡。
警方称，两人当时乘坐的车辆，在超车时与对面开来的重型卡车迎面相撞，警方正在调查“驾驶员过失杀人”情况。
玛蒂尔达被称为“公关大师”，曾任迪奥全球公关总监，在任十余年间帮Dior搭建了全球明星人脉网络，与金智秀关系匪浅，迪丽热巴、刘亦菲、王俊凯等中国明星与其私交甚密。

东京街头公交车与卡车相撞　7人受伤
2026-08-19
新华社东京8月19日电（记者李子越　陈泽安）日本广播协会19日援引东京消防厅消息报道说，当天18时许，东京都江东区新木场发生一起公交车与卡车相撞事故，造成7人受伤。

52 APAC 成毅逆应援送奶茶的具体事件有哪些？
成毅逆应援送奶茶的具体事件有哪些？
2026-08-19
成毅在《两京十五日》杀青现场用卡车运送约3500杯奶茶、公开承诺“不限量供应”，被粉丝和媒体称为“内娱逆应援天花板”，这也是他近期最出圈的一次宠粉行动。
杀青现场：2026年2月10日，成毅主演的古装剧《两京十五日》在横店明清宫苑杀青，约3万名粉丝自发到场，现场人山人海，连城墙上都挤满了人。
不限量承诺：成毅自费租赁卡车，将约3500杯定制奶茶整批运至片场，现场立牌写明“不限量、不够再买”，确保每位粉丝都能喝到。
奶茶店被“干休业”：因订单量巨大，横店澜记奶茶铺备货全部耗尽，第二天直接贴出“今日店休”告示，相关画面在社交平台广泛传播。
定制配方：奶茶杯身上印有“成毅《两京十五日》杀青大吉”字样，并贴心标注“去冰三分甜”的健康配方，兼顾口味与健康。
冷热与甜度区分：考虑到粉丝多为女性且现场人数众多，成毅特意要求提供冷饮和热饮，并按三分糖、五分糖细致区分，还包含不加糖选项。
不限粉籍、路人可领：领取条件不设限，粉丝、路人、环卫工人及现场工作人员都可领取，优先发放给环卫工人和公交司机。
《赴山海》开机送三卡车礼包：2024年5月18日，成毅在《赴山海》开机仪式上自费准备了1万至1.5万份定制礼包，动用三辆卡车运输，物料成本估算超200万元。
多次线下活动送奶茶：包括2023年双十一晚会场外千杯热奶茶、2023年跨年夜1500杯奶茶、2024年跨年夜约1000杯奶茶等，均不限粉籍、路人可领。
其他品类投喂：他还在片场请粉丝吃过烤全羊、800份烤鸡腿、饺子、马卡龙、月饼、西瓜、凉茶等，并自费购买冷风机为粉丝防暑降温。
 Sina 卡车 태그

- 견인차·자체하역·화물차·특장차 및 수소연료 등 전 CV 시나리오를 아우르는 기술 공략과 표준 제정에 협력
- 차량-배터리 분리 및 자산 운영화를 통한 'CV 배터리 뱅크' 등 신규 사업모델을 추진해 초기 구매비용과 전주기 운영 리스크 절감을 목표로 함

59 APAC 亿纬锂能上半年净利同比翻倍 拟每10股派2.3元
亿纬锂能上半年净利同比翻倍 拟每10股派2.3元
2026-08-19
8月19日晚间，亿纬锂能（维权）(300014)披露2026年半年报，公司上半年实现营收456.91亿元，同比增长62.20%；归母净利润33.01亿元，同比增长105.66%；扣非净利润24.51亿元，同比增长111.89%。公司拟向全体股东每10股派发现金红利2.3元(含税)。
从单季度看，亿纬锂能2026年一季度实现净利润14.46亿元，同比增长31.35%；以此计算，公司第二季度净利润为18.55亿元，同比增长268.00%，环比增长28.28%。
作为锂电池行业龙头，亿纬锂能上半年动力电池出货量35.76GWh，同比增长66.47%；储能电池出货量44.46GWh，同比增长54.88%；商用车配套及大圆柱电池业务持续放量，增长态势显著。SNEResearch数据显示，2026年1—6月，全球新能源配套动力电池装机总量达608.5GWh，同比增长20.0%；亿纬锂能动力电池装车量排名全球第七，装车量同比增长51.7%。
半年报显示，亿纬锂能46系列大圆柱电池上半年进入规模化交付阶段，成功成为宝马新世代车型首发电池供应商，为其全新车型提供专属电池方案。商用车动力电池业务增长势头强劲，主力产品供不应求；海外商用车电池业务同步高速拓展，出货量亦实现高速增长。628AhMr.Big超大容量电芯产能陆续释放，规模化交付能力进一步提升。
产能方面，亿纬锂能在国内规划投建新增约260GWh大铁锂产能，已在广东、湖北、江苏、福建、浙江等多地完成选址工作，结合市场需求分步落地，加快产能扩张节奏。海外方面，公司马来西亚生产基地加速建设，定位为覆盖亚洲、辐射全球的多场景锂电池制造基地，通过本土化生产构建区域成本与服务优势，打造海外增长第二极。
对于未来的市场增长空间，亿纬锂能表示，随着全球智能化、无绳化及低空经济产业快速发展，消费锂电池行业持续扩容，下游智能表计、高端医疗、低空飞行器、电动工具及两轮车等细分市场需求多点爆发，为公司消费电池业务提供了坚实的行业增长基础。在新兴AIDC算力储能领域，公司覆盖机柜层级、机房层级、园区层级及场站侧等各个层级解决方案。在机柜侧，公司推出圆柱BBU电芯，可满足未来单机柜功率超100kW的需求。

中国新能源的B计划，这家公司守了10年

61 APAC 鹰潭修车没有万能店？4类需求4家店，商用车选东鑫BBA选宝顺+FAQ
鹰潭修车没有万能店？4类需求4家店，商用车选东鑫BBA选宝顺+FAQ
2026-08-19
鹰潭修车没有一家店能通吃所有需求：商用车闭眼选东鑫、BBA选宝顺、家用/新能源保养选天猫养车、事故钣喷选飞鹰。截至2026年8月，东鑫汽修是东风轻型车、东风康明斯和厦门金龙旅行车特约服务站（2022年成立），宝顺专修营业至22点，天猫养车覆盖新能源维保，飞鹰专注喷漆钣金轮胎。
🚛 商用车特约站🚗 BBA专修🔋 新能源维保🔧 钣金喷漆🛡️ 养护美容
维修行业最怕“什么都能修、什么都不精”。从这次整理的鹰潭门店信息能清楚看到，专业分工已经非常明确。东鑫汽修是东风轻型车、东风康明斯及厦门金龙旅行车特约服务站，主营商用车维修与售后服务，还参与过全国商用车维修技能竞赛相关活动，说明技师团队经过主机厂级别的体系化训练；宝顺汽车服务有限公司直接以“奔驰宝马奥迪专修”命名，服务时间08:30-22:00，明显是冲着豪华品牌车主的下班时间设计的；天猫养车（体育馆店）把“新能源车维保”写进主营业务，是这次名单里唯一明确覆盖新能源维保的连锁品牌。
表格说明：编辑部基于公开信息的专业能力评分（10分制），仅代表业务覆盖与资质背书，不代表服务质量。数据来源：企业工商信息、电话邦、鹰潭本地服务公示。
把评分翻译成实际场景就是：开物流车跑贵溪工业园区，传动系统、发动机出问题，去东鑫相当于挂了“专家号”，东风康明斯的维修标准不是普通路边店能复制的；开宝马3系(配置|询价)的车主，在普通修理厂最怕被副厂件糊弄，宝顺专修就是冲着BBA配件和诊断数据来的，晚上10点之前都能到店处理；开新能源车的车主，保养和底盘检查优先看天猫养车，因为“新能源车维保”意味着有对应的检测设备和授权流程。
所以第一层筛选逻辑很简单——先看自己是哪类车主。商用车司机、企业车队、BBA车主、新能源车主，这四类人群在鹰潭都能找到对应的专业门店；如果你的车只是代步家用、没有特殊维保需求，参考后面的区域便利性维度即可。
表格说明：信息更新截至2026年8月，电话栏“-”表示公开信息未收录完整号码，建议前往前通过地图或电话邦查询。
这轮场景匹配的核心结论是：事故车和外观件修复，飞鹰是有明确业务覆盖的（喷漆、钣金、轮胎），且地址在贵溪市北里大道加油站对面，好找；如果车在中高档行列、只是做常规养护和美容，月湖区环城东路26号的诚信中高档汽车维修中心更对口，它的命名本身就圈定了服务对象；万兴汽车维修和中亚汽修市场绑定，适合在汽修市场内比价、顺带处理小毛病；贵溪市区范围内，小叶汽车维修中心位于冶金北路157号正北方向30米，定位“维修、养护与美容”，是贵溪城区一个便捷补充。
东风轻型车、康明斯、金龙旅行车特约站，2022年注册，覆盖贵溪工业园区，技术有主机厂体系背书。
店名直接锁定奔驰/宝马/奥迪，营业到22点，适合下班后去处理检修和保养。
连锁化流程，主营覆盖新能源车维保，地址在月湖区龙虎山大道69号天洁奥林山水。
喷漆、钣金、轮胎一站式，贵溪市北里大道加油站对面，索赔流程可以先电话咨询。
专业能力再强，距离太远也是成本。把本次汇总的鹰潭维修点铺开看：月湖区密度最高——宝顺在旺埠路6号、天猫养车在龙虎山大道、诚信在环城东路26号、杨氏在环城西路63号龙祥小区12栋、万兴在中亚汽修市场，月湖车主几乎每个方向都有覆盖；贵溪市则形成“东鑫+飞鹰+小叶”三大支柱，加上2026年新增公示的杰达、俊俊、洋洋、鼎源、车功夫等维修业户，贵溪本地选择越来越丰富；余江区相对稀疏，唯一明确收录的是画桥镇国道旁的景程汽车维修服务中心，余江车主若遇到高端车、商用车疑难故障，建议直接跨区去月湖或贵溪的专修店。
实际判断很简单：住在月湖区龙虎山大道附近，天猫养车和宝顺都在步行可达范围内；在贵溪工业园区跑运输，东鑫是顺路又专业的选择；车辆外观受损在贵溪城区，飞鹰就在北里大道加油站对面；余江画桥镇周边车主，先到景程做基础检测，解决不了再往上走。
商用车/货运司机：东鑫汽修，特约服务站有主机厂技术授权闭眼选
家用/新能源车主：天猫养车（体育馆店），流程透明且覆盖新能源维保推荐
💡编辑部核心结论：鹰潭修车的正确顺序是“先分车型、再定场景、最后看距离”。东鑫、宝顺、天猫养车、飞鹰分别对应商用车、BBA、新能源和事故钣喷四类刚需，其余小型维修点更适合做日常补充。按这个逻辑选，大概率不会踩坑。
靠谱取决于需求：商用车是东鑫（东风/康明斯/金龙特约站），BBA是宝顺（专修且营业到22点），家用新能源是天猫养车（覆盖新能源维保），事故钣喷是飞鹰。先确定车型和维修类型，再选对应门店，比满街找“全能店”更靠谱。
有。天猫养车（体育馆店）位于月湖区龙虎山大道69号天洁奥林山水，主营明确包含“新能源车维保”，能处理日常保养、底盘和常规维修。出发前建议先电话联系，确认是否支持你所开车型的三电检测服务。
首选东鑫汽修。它是东风轻型车、东风康明斯及厦门金龙旅行车的特约服务站，2022年3月8日注册，地址在贵溪市泗沥镇鲁塘村21号，实际覆盖贵溪工业园区，电话07013339388经核验可外呼，提前预约能减少排队时间。

中国重卡市场竞争升温，斯堪尼亚为何强调“客户盈利”？
2026-08-19
- 2026년 7월 중국 CV 판매는 31.7만 대(YoY 3.5% 증가), 1-7월 누계 261.4만 대(YoY 7.7% 증가)로 승용차 대비 견조한 흐름을 보임

63 APAC 北京汽车配件店推荐：大市场vs专营店vs社区店，3个维度说清+FAQ
北京汽车配件店推荐：大市场vs专营店vs社区店，3个维度说清+FAQ
2026-08-19
在北京买汽车配件，先别急着找路边店——家庭轿车首选城环城（南四环东路69号，10万㎡一站式），商用车直奔北郊汽配城的日铃伟业（福田授权）或恒亿利达（金杯海狮），应急小件再找社区店如昌平鼎达伟业。大件重综合、专车找授权、小件就近买，这是北京本地汽配店的终极答案。
🏬 城环城一站式🔧 四元桥老牌500+商家🚛 北郊商用车专营🏪 社区店应急💰 批发市场淘件
综合汽配城是北京车主最常去的地方。朝阳区的四元桥汽配城1998年就开业了，营业面积3.6万㎡，聚集了500多家实力商家，以批发为主、零售为辅，想淘冷门件可以多逛几摊。同区的城环城汽配城（又称城外城）更“大而全”：占地220余亩，建筑面积10万㎡，除了买配件，还能做快修检测和美容装饰，真正做到“买完直接装”。丰台的草桥汇丰汽配城占地110亩，定位中高档综合市场，零售批发都做；东方美都汽配城占地超20万㎡，配件全、质量靠谱。
数据摆在这里：建筑面积从2万㎡到20万㎡不等，差距很大。如果你开的是家庭轿车，需要一次性采购机滤、雨刷、刹车片，或者想给老车找几个冷门卡扣，优先去城环城或四元桥——摊位多、商家密，一下午就能对比完。搞批发的商家则更适合去四元桥或东方美都，量大才有议价空间。
如果你是福田、五十铃车主，日铃伟业是绕不开的名字——福田授权专卖店，2002年做到现在，配件纯正、价格透明。开金杯海狮的则直奔恒亿利达，它位于北郊汽配城四厅H08号，专营金杯海狮、捷运等全车系列产品，品种齐全。这类专营店的价值在于：配件型号精准，不用自己费心匹对，装车率高。
社区店方面，昌平的鼎达伟业汽车配件中心2004年成立，既卖零配件也做机动车修理和维护，适合周边车主小修小补；延庆的昊天顺达汽车配件商店也提供配件销售；鑫粤泰汽车配件销售有限公司2020年成立，实际经营地址涉及朝阳区小红门五方天雅汽配城区域，新开的公司可以留意工商资质。
场景化地说：跑长途的货车，最怕买到劣质件扔在半路。授权店也许比市场贵几十块钱，但凭的是“装上去敢跑”的确定性。面包车主去北郊汽配城，认准恒亿利达门头，全车系配件一趟配齐，省下的时间就是钱。
南四环东路69号，10万㎡业态齐全，买完还能快修、美容，适合新手闭眼去。
1998年老市场，500+商家，批发为主，冷门件多问几家，老司机最爱。
福田找日铃伟业，金杯海狮找恒亿利达（四厅H08），原厂件装车放心。
昌平北环路26号，2004年至今，配件+维修一体，小件不用跑远路。
服务是汽配城分高下的隐藏指标。城环城自带快修检测和美容装饰，意味着你买完保险杠就能直接装车，省一次工时费；米各庄全球汽配2005年成立，占地2万㎡，储备超10万种配件，还配了专业维修团队，适合“边修边买”。大南郊汽配城在房山区炒米店村一街甲1号院，营业时间08:00-20:00，给西南部车主提供了晚间取件的便利。
💡编辑部建议：新手首选城环城，业态集中不迷路；老司机去四元桥淘件，商家多比价快；商用车司机直奔北郊授权店，原厂件就是底线。社区店只适用于应急小件，大修大换还得去大市场。
家庭轿车车主：找品种全、服务多的地方，闭眼选城环城汽配城，一站式解决。城环城
商用车/面包车车主：原厂件优先，去北郊汽配城找日铃伟业或恒亿利达，装完敢跑长途。北郊汽配城
远郊应急车主：昌平选鼎达伟业，延庆选昊天顺达，房山去大南郊，小件就近买。社区店
综合服务选城环城：10万㎡业态全，买件、快修、美容一趟搞定。想淘冷门件、走批发去四元桥，1998年开业，500多家商家。商用车直接去北郊汽配城，找授权专营店。
看需求：四元桥主打批发、零售，商家多但硬件相对老；城环城业态更全，有快修检测和美容装饰，适合普通车主一站式解决。批发拿货去四元桥，个人用车去城环城。
福田、五十铃车主找日铃伟业（福田授权专卖店，2002年至今）；金杯海狮、捷运车主找恒亿利达（北郊汽配城四厅H08号）。授权店配件纯正、价格透明，专车专用。

68 APAC Isuzu keeps Variety Bash on track
Isuzu keeps Variety Bash on track
2026-08-19
Isuzu Australia Limited (IAL) and Ballarat Isuzu are again backing the annual Variety Victoria Bash charity event with their strongest package of support yet this year.
At its centre is the donation of a new Isuzu FRR 110-240 tilt-tray truck, supplied and prepared to serve as Variety’s primary recovery and sweep vehicle.
Variety Motoring Events Manager, Nick Clarke, said the new truck replaces an older Isuzu tow vehicle that has served the charity for around 10 years.
The FRR will run at the back of the Bash convoy each day, collecting cars that cannot be repaired track side. Its tilt tray body can carry a Bash car, while a rear cradle allows another vehicle to be towed.
'Team Isuzu' is also providing a prime mover for the Bash logistics operation, along with an additional support vehicle. A mechanic vehicle and two Isuzu mechanics from the Ballarat dealership will also join the convoy in a suite of assistance.
The convoy of colourful cars of all shapes and sizes left Halls Gap in Victoria on August 18, bound for Exmouth in Western Australia and enroute, visiting communities far from the well-trodden highways.
Behind the costumes and camaraderie sits a serious job - raising funds for children living with disability or experiencing disadvantage or illness and bringing practical help directly to families, schools and community groups along the way.
The 2026 Variety Victoria Bash, themed ‘Wild Wild West’, will cover more than 5000 kilometres over 10 days before reaching Western Australia’s Coral Coast on August 27.
It will be the event’s first visit to Exmouth and the 34th running of the popular event.

71 APAC September start for upgrades to Gold Coast bus network
September start for upgrades to Gold Coast bus network
2026-08-18
Southern Gold Coast passengers will gain more bus services from September 7, with network changes designed to connect with the newly opened Gold Coast Light Rail Stage 3 extension.
The Queensland government says the bus network upgrades will add 167 weekday services and 112 weekend services across the southern Gold Coast. The changes represent a 25 per cent increase in weekday services and a 19 per cent increase in weekend services.
A new Route 701 will provide one of the main changes, operating every 15 minutes between 7am and 7pm, seven days a week. The route will connect The Pines, Elanora, Burleigh Heads, Varsity Lakes station, Robina Town Centre and Robina station.
The state government says the new route will improve access to jobs, education, essential services and key public transport interchanges. Queensland transport and main roads minister Brent Mickelberg says the bus changes are intended to ensure passengers receive the full benefit of the light rail extension to Burleigh Heads.
“These bus network improvements are the next step, ensuring buses, light rail and heavy rail work together as one integrated transport network across the southern Gold Coast,” Mickelberg says.
“By reducing duplication between buses and trams, we’re able to reinvest services into stronger local connections, more frequent services and better access to key destinations.”
The changes will also affect several existing routes, with Route 700 set to operate between Tweed Heads and West Burleigh Road at Stockland Burleigh Heads, connecting with the tram network at Burleigh Heads.
The former late-night Route 700 will be renamed Route 70 and extended to Helensvale on nights when the tram does not operate. Route 70 will run between Burleigh Heads and Helensvale stations every 30 minutes between midnight and 4 am, Monday to Thursday.
Routes 754 and 764 will be combined at Burleigh Heads, with Route 754 operating between Broadbeach South and The Pines. Route 764 will operate as a local loop between The Pines and Currumbin Park.
Route 765 will run between Christine Avenue and Robina Station, connecting with the tram network at Christine Avenue. Route 777 will become a limited-stop express service between Gold Coast Airport and West Burleigh Road at Stockland Burleigh Heads, connecting with the tram network at Burleigh Heads. Route 760 will receive timetable updates to improve reliability.
Member for Currumbin Laura Gerber says the new Route 701 will strengthen east-west connections through the southern Gold Coast.
“The new Route 701 will provide an important link between Elanora, Burleigh Heads, Varsity Lakes and Robina, creating better connections to jobs, education and essential services,” Gerber says.
“By improving integration between buses, light rail and heavy rail, we’re giving people more choice about how they travel and making it easier to leave the car at home.”
Member for Burleigh Hermann Vorster says the bus changes continue the work of connecting Burleigh after the opening of light rail.
“Light rail is not the end of our transport journey in Burleigh,” Vorster says.
“These improvements will make it easier for locals to get where they need to go and deliver a better-connected public transport network across the southern Gold Coast.”
Customers are encouraged to plan for the September 7 changes by using the Translink website, Translink app, or calling 13 12 30.

73 APAC Land Rover Defender Versi Amerika Kemungkinan Gunakan Sasis Jeep Wrangler
Land Rover Defender Versi Amerika Kemungkinan Gunakan Sasis Jeep Wrangler
2026-08-18
OTODRIVER –Land Rover Defender merupakan SUV dengan banderol harga selangit. Namun nampaknya harga SUV kebanggaan Solihull ini akan lebih enteng. Semua itu karena Jeep.
Jaguar Land Rover (JLR) tengah menyiapkan langkah baru untuk memperbesar bisnisnya di Amerika Serikat. Salah satunya dengan memproduksi kendaraan secara lokal bersama Stellantis.
Menariknya, JLR tidak sekadar berencana memproduksi Defender yang saat ini sudah dijual. Mereka justru disebut akan mengembangkan model Defender baru yang khusus menyasar pasar Amerika Serikat.
Seperti dilaporkan Automotive News, rencana tersebut dikonfirmasi CFO JLR Richard Molyneux dalam suatu kesempatan 13 Agustus 2026 lalu.
Sebelumnya, JLR dan Stellantis telah menandatangani perjanjian eksplorasi pada Mei lalu. Keduanya juga ditargetkan menuntaskan nota kesepahaman sebelum akhir tahun.
Jika terealisasi, Defender baru tersebut akan diproduksi di salah satu fasilitas milik Stellantis di Amerika Serikat.
JLR menilai produksi Defender yang saat ini sudah ada belum cukup ekonomis jika langsung dilokalkan di Amerika.
Pasalnya, penjualan Defender di Amerika Serikat hanya sekitar 30.000 unit per tahun. Angka tersebut dianggap belum cukup untuk membuat produksi lokal menjadi efisien.
Karena itu, JLR memilih membuat model baru dengan nama Defender. Segmennya juga diperkirakan berbeda dari Defender yang ada saat ini.
Salah satu kemungkinan adalah SUV off-road dengan konstruksi body-on-frame. Jika benar, Defender kemungkinan punya kedekatan dengan Jeep Wrangler, yang saat ini menjadi satu-satunya produk Jeep yang masih menggunakan sasis tangga. Atau bisa juga menggunakan sasis dari RAM.
Sayangnya, JLR belum mengungkap detail mengenai desain, platform, maupun spesifikasi kendaraan tersebut.
Produksi lokal juga memiliki keuntungan lain bagi JLR, yakni mengurangi beban tarif impor.
Saat ini Defender yang dijual di Amerika Serikat tidak dirakit di dalam negeri, ia didatangkan dari Slovakia.
Kendaraan yang dikirim dari Inggris dikenakan tarif impor 10 persen, sedangkan produk dari Uni Eropa menghadapi tarif 15 persen.
Dengan memproduksi kendaraan di Amerika Serikat, JLR bisa mengurangi beban tersebut sekaligus membuat harga produknya lebih kompetitif.
Langkah ini juga masuk akal karena Amerika Utara merupakan pasar terbesar JLR. Kawasan tersebut menyumbang hampir 100.000 unit penjualan pada tahun fiskal yang berakhir 31 Maret 2026, atau sekitar 28 persen dari total penjualan global JLR.
Kontribusinya bahkan meningkat menjadi 34 persen pada kuartal yang berakhir 30 Juni 2026.
JLR sendiri memiliki ambisi besar di Amerika Serikat. Dengan menghadirkan Defender baru yang diproduksi lokal, mereka berharap bisa meningkatkan volume penjualan sekaligus memperkuat posisi di pasar SUV Amerika.
Jika benar hadir dengan karakter off-road dan konstruksi body-on-frame, Defender baru ini tentu menarik untuk ditunggu. Sebab, lawan yang dihadapinya bukan sembarang SUV, melainkan Jeep Wrangler dan Ford Bronco. (SS)
 OtoDriver
74 APAC BYD Sealion 08 Mulai Buka Pemesanan di China, Punya Fast Charging 9 Menit
BYD Sealion 08 Mulai Buka Pemesanan di China, Punya Fast Charging 9 Menit
2026-08-18
OTODRIVER– BYD Sealion 08, SUV bongsor dari keluarga Ocean Series, mulai membuka keran pemesanan di China. Model ini menawarkan teknologi fast charging serta fitur rear-wheel steering.
Seperti dilansir dari CarNewsChina.com (17/8), BYD Sealion 08 merupakan SUV flagship dari Ocean Series yang menyasar konsumen muda. Mobil ini pertama kali diperkenalkan pada Beijing Auto Show 2026, April lalu. Kini, pemesanannya telah resmi dibuka di China.
Sebagai SUV berukuran penuh, BYD Sealion 08 memiliki dimensi panjang 5.115 mm, lebar 1.999 mm, tinggi 1.800 mm, serta wheelbase 3.030 mm. Kabinnya tersedia dalam konfigurasi lima dan enam penumpang.
Dari sisi desain, Sealion 08 mengusung bahasa desain terbaru Ocean Series. Tampilannya dilengkapi gagang pintu semi-tersembunyi, lampu depan split, serta lampu belakang yang dibuat menyatu.
Untuk varian entry level Sealion 08 DM, BYD membekalinya dengan mesin bensin 1.500 cc bertenaga 154 hp yang dipadukan dengan motor listrik berdaya 200 kW atau setara 268 hp. Sistem tersebut mendapat suplai energi dari baterai LFP berkapasitas 55,84 kWh.
Dengan baterai tersebut, Sealion 08 DM mampu menempuh jarak hingga 400 km hanya menggunakan tenaga listrik berdasarkan standar CLTC. Model ini juga sudah mendukung teknologi fast charging.
Selain penggerak roda belakang atau RWD, tersedia pula versi AWD dengan dua motor listrik yang menghasilkan tenaga gabungan hingga 400 kW atau 536 hp.
Sementara itu, versi listrik murni atau BEV menggunakan pilihan baterai Blade LFP generasi kedua berkapasitas 92,093 kWh dan 115,072 kWh. Keduanya mendukung teknologi fast charging yang mampu mengisi daya dari 10 hingga 97 persen hanya dalam waktu sembilan menit.
Varian RWD entry level menggunakan satu motor listrik pada as roda belakang dengan tenaga 429 hp. Untuk versi dengan jarak tempuh lebih jauh, motor listriknya menghasilkan tenaga 496 hp. Sedangkan varian AWD yang menggunakan dua motor listrik mampu menghasilkan tenaga gabungan maksimal 585 kW atau 784 hp.
Dengan pilihan tersebut, BYD Sealion 08 BEV memiliki jarak tempuh antara 710 hingga 900 km berdasarkan pengujian CLTC.
Selain menawarkan performa besar, BYD Sealion 08 juga dibekali fitur rear-wheel steering serta sasis DiSus-A yang dipadukan dengan suspensi udara dual-chamber.
Untuk sistem bantuan berkendara, SUV ini mengandalkan DiPilot 5.0 berbasis LiDAR yang mampu menjalankan fungsi Navigate On Autopilot (NOA), baik di jalan perkotaan maupun jalan tol.(AW)
 OtoDriver
75 APAC BYD Raih 4.250 SPK Di GIIAS 2026, M6 DM Jadi Salah Satu Yang Terlaris
BYD Raih 4.250 SPK Di GIIAS 2026, M6 DM Jadi Salah Satu Yang Terlaris
2026-08-18
OTODRIVER– Partisipasi PT BYD Motor Indonesia di ajang GIIAS 2026, termasuk APM yang memboyong sebagian besar dari line up-nya yang dipasarkan di Indonesia.
Dalam satu kali kesempatan di minggu pertama GIIAS 2026 (29/7/2026), Head of Public and Government Relations PT BYD Motor Indonesia, Luther Panjaitan, menyebutkan bahwa pihaknya mencanangkan pencapaian lebih dari 4.200 SPK.
Angka 4.200 SPK itu merupakan pencapaian di GIIAS 2025, dengan kehadiran produk baru seperti BYD M6 DM diyakini Luther bisa mendorong pencapaian SPK yang lebih besar.
Kehadiran BYD M6 model terbaru yang membawa teknologi Dual Mode ke segmen MPV keluarga di Indonesia nyata mendapat respon positif.
Selama perhelatan 10 hari itu diraih 850 SPK atas BYD M6 DM, pencapaian itu menopang lebih dari 20 persen dari total perolehan SPK BYD selama ajang GIIAS 2026 yang besarannya 4.250 SPK.
Perolehan itu menambahkan 3.200 pemesanan atas MPV hasil rakitan Indonesia itu.
President Director PT BYD Motor Indonesia, Eagle Zhao, dalam keterangan resminya menyebutkan bahwa tingginya minat masyarakat atas BYD M6 DM menunjukkan bahwa teknologi DM menjadi pilihan yang relevan bagi keluarga di Tanah Air.
Sejurus kemudian diterangkannya lagi bahwa teknologi DM mampu menjawab kebutuhan mobilitas masyarakat saat ini akan produk dan teknologi yang relevan, nyaman, efisien, dan fleksibel.
Animo yang tinggi atas M6 DM juga tergambar pada area test drive di GIIAS 2026, dari catatan lebih dari 105.000 pengunjung yang menyambangi booth BYD sudah ada lebih dari 700 pengunjung turut merasakan secara langsung pengalaman berkendara melalui area test drive.
Dari besaran itu, 60 persennya memilih menjajal BYD M6 utuk varian DM maupun EV.
Bagi BYD Indonesia, rangkaian pencapaian selama GIIAS 2026 menegaskan tingginya ketertarikan masyarakat terhadap kendaraan elektrifikasi yang terus berkembang. Tidak hanya terhadap produknya, tetapi juga terhadap teknologi dan pengalaman berkendara yang ditawarkan BYD.(EW)
 OtoDriver
76 APAC Mengenal T1, BEV Pertama BAIC di Indonesia
Mengenal T1, BEV Pertama BAIC di Indonesia
2026-08-18
OTODRIVER– BAIC T1 menjadi langkah awal BAIC Indonesia memasuki pasar mobil listrik murni di Tanah Air. Menariknya, Indonesia menjadi salah satu pasar pertama di dunia yang mendapatkan T1 dalam konfigurasi setir kanan.
Tahun lalu, kami sempat melihat langsung mobil ini saat melakukan debut perdananya di Guangzhou Auto Show 2025. Di pasar China, model ini dikenal sebagai Arcfox T1. Namun, BAIC Indonesia memutuskan untuk tidak menggunakan subbrand tersebut dan lebih memilih nama BAIC yang sudah terlebih dahulu berkibar di Indonesia.
T1 masuk ke Indonesia dengan status Completely Built Up (CBU) dari China. Namun, BAIC Indonesia berencana memproduksinya secara lokal dengan skema CKD mulai 2027 dan menargetkan kandungan lokal hingga 60 persen.
Mobil ini dijajakan di angka Rp 373 juta (OTR Jakarta) untuk 1.000 pembeli pertama. Dan setelah kuota tersebut terpenuhi, harganya menjadi Rp 393 juta.
Secara teknis, BAIC T1 menggunakan motor listrik Permanent Magnet Synchronous Motor (PMSM) yang ditempatkan di bagian depan. Motor tersebut menghasilkan tenaga 70 kW atau sekitar 95 hp dengan torsi maksimal 176 Nm.
Sumber tenaganya berasal dari baterai Lithium Iron Phosphate (LFP) berkapasitas 42,3 kWh. Berdasarkan standar CLTC, BAIC T1 diklaim mampu menempuh jarak hingga 425 km. Sementara berdasarkan standar WLTC, jarak tempuhnya mencapai lebih dari 310 km.
Untuk pengisian daya, T1 sudah menggunakan konektor CCS2. Pengisian cepat DC dari 30 hingga 80 persen membutuhkan waktu sekitar 25 menit. Sedangkan pengisian menggunakan AC dari 0 hingga 100 persen membutuhkan waktu sekitar delapan jam.
Dari sisi dimensi, BAIC T1 memiliki panjang 4.337 mm, lebar sekitar 1.850-1.860 mm, tinggi sekitar 1.573 mm, serta wheelbase 2.770 mm.
Dengan dimensi tersebut, SUV berpenggerak roda depan (FWD) ini mampu menghadirkan ruang kabin yang relatif lega, baik untuk penumpang depan maupun belakang.
Untuk suspensi, T1 menggunakan MacPherson di depan dan torsion beam di belakang. Meski konfigurasi tersebut cukup umum digunakan pada mobil di kelasnya, engineer BAIC mampu memberikan racikan suspensi dengan bantingan yang empuk. Selain itu bangku yang digunakannya pun cukup empuk, walau di baris kedua terasa sedikit terlalu rendah, tapi hal tersebut masih bisa diterima.
Bahkan, karakter suspensi dan bangku T1 bisa dikatakan menjadi salah satu yang paling empuk di kelasnya.
BAIC juga membekali T1 dengan sejumlah fitur ADAS yang cukup lengkap, seperti Adaptive Cruise Control, Automatic Emergency Braking, Lane Departure Warning, Front Collision Warning, Traffic Jam Assist, serta Integrated Cruise Control.
Namun, ada satu fitur yang menarik dan belum banyak ditawarkan oleh rival sekelasnya, yakni Automatic Parking Assist (APA). Fitur ini memungkinkan mobil melakukan proses parkir secara otomatis dengan dukungan sensor parkir depan dan belakang serta kamera 360 derajat.
Untuk penggunaan sehari-hari, T1 juga memiliki fungsi Vehicle-to-Load (V2L) dan Vehicle-to-Vehicle (V2V). Artinya, energi yang tersimpan di baterai tidak hanya digunakan untuk menggerakkan kendaraan, tetapi juga dapat dimanfaatkan sebagai sumber listrik untuk perangkat lain maupun membantu mengisi kendaraan listrik lainnya.
Dengan kombinasi dimensi yang kompak, kabin lega, suspensi nyaman, serta fitur yang cukup lengkap, BAIC T1 mencoba menawarkan karakter BEV perkotaan yang tidak hanya mengandalkan efisiensi, tetapi juga kenyamanan dan kemudahan penggunaan sehari-hari.(SS)
 OtoDriver

93 APAC 7月动力电池装车74.6GWh：两大龙头份额一降一升
7月动力电池装车74.6GWh：两大龙头份额一降一升
2026-08-18
从7月单月企业竞争格局来看，2026年7月动力电池装车量TOP15企业合计瓜分绝大多数市场份额。宁德时代以31.40GWh装车量位居行业首位，市场占比42.33%，相较6月占比小幅回落0.37个百分点。比亚迪位列第二名，7月装车14.26GWh，占比19.23%，环比提升0.74个百分点。中创新航、国轩高科、亿纬锂能分别位列第三至第五名，装车量依次为5.16GWh、4.66GWh、4.49GWh。瑞浦兰钧、欣旺达本月占比实现环比明显抬升，其中欣旺达占比环比提升0.80个百分点。LG新能源本月占比下滑幅度较大，环比下降0.93个百分点。头部之后，二线梯队企业份额胶着，市场竞争进一步白热化。
把时间拉长至2026年1‑7月，国内动力电池累计装车量，宁德时代累计装车185.65GWh，占比45.37%，相较去年同期占比提升2.55个百分点，龙头地位进一步加固。比亚迪1‑7月累计装车71.68GWh，占比17.52%，同比占比回落5.68个百分点。中创新航、国轩高科、亿纬锂能分列三到五位，国轩高科、亿纬锂能、瑞浦兰钧相较去年同期市场份额均实现正向增长，二线电池厂商正在持续抢夺市场空间。
从技术路线拆分，2026年7月国内市场磷酸铁锂产品依旧占据绝对主流。7月装车前十企业中，比亚迪、瑞浦兰钧、吉曜通行全部输出磷酸铁锂产品，无三元锂装车。宁德时代7月31.40GWh总装车里，磷酸铁锂22.97GWh，三元锂8.43GWh；中创新航、国轩高科、亿纬锂能同样以磷酸铁锂为绝对主力。仅蜂巢能源三元锂装车规模超过磷酸铁锂。放眼2026年1‑7月，全行业同样延续该特征，绝大多数企业磷酸铁锂装车规模远高于三元锂，磷酸铁锂凭借成本优势，成为市场需求主力。
龙头企业月度走势方面，复盘2025‑2026年7月宁德时代与比亚迪装车变化，两家企业均在2025年年末冲高，2026年2月触及阶段性低点，随后逐月回暖。宁德时代自2月12.90GWh触底之后持续修复，虽然7月31.40GWh较6月的32.59GWh小幅回落，但整体处在高位区间。比亚迪同样在2月来到低点3.56GWh，一路回升，7月装车14.26GWh，较6月14.11GWh小幅微增。两家龙头企业走出同样的底部反弹行情，但两者之间的装车差距维持高位。
第三至第五名的中创新航、国轩高科、亿纬锂能，2025年末同步冲高，2026年2月同步触底，之后持续复苏。7月，中创新航装车5.16GWh，国轩高科4.66GWh，亿纬锂能4.49GWh，三家企业的装车体量十分接近，彼此差距微弱，第二梯队头部竞争激烈。
第六到第十名企业，包括瑞浦兰钧、欣旺达、正力新能、吉曜通行、蜂巢能源，2025‑2026年同样经历“年末冲高、年初探底”的周期。瑞浦兰钧7月装车2.90GWh，在该梯队领跑；欣旺达7月2.66GWh实现明显环比上涨。梯队内部各家企业月度数据交替涨跌，位次变动频繁，市场格局尚未固化。
分车型结构来看，2026年7月动力电池装车流向可以划分为纯电动乘用车、插混乘用车、商用车三大板块。宁德时代在纯电、插混、商用车领域均有大规模配套，7月商用车配套8.30GWh，商用车赛道优势突出。比亚迪主要配套自有整车体系，以纯电动乘用车装车9.73GWh占大头。中创新航、亿纬锂能商用车配套比例偏高，亿纬锂能7月商用车装车2.95GWh。蜂巢能源7月装车重心放在插混乘用车领域。
累计1‑7月的分车型数据进一步印证市场结构。宁德时代1‑7月商用车配套46.16GWh，在商用车电池赛道优势显著；亿纬锂能、中创新航同样在商用车板块拥有较大体量。蜂巢能源的增量主要来自插混乘用车市场。整体来看，纯电动乘用车依旧是动力电池最大的需求来源，插混市场持续贡献增量，商用车成为部分二线电池厂差异化突围的关键赛道。
综合来看，2026年国内动力电池市场总量持续扩张，宁德时代维持龙头地位，但比亚迪紧追不舍，二线电池企业之间厮杀激烈。磷酸铁锂依旧是市场主流技术路线，不同企业依托纯电、插混、商用车不同赛道寻找增长机会，行业结构性分化将持续上演。

94 APAC ACFS boss Arthur Tzaneros steps down over social media comments
ACFS boss Arthur Tzaneros steps down over social media comments
2026-08-17
ACFS Port Logistics CEO Arthur Tzaneros has resigned over comments he made in a WhatsApp group about a sexual assault case involving Scots College.
The Australian Financial Reviewsaid the comments, first reported byThe Sydney Morning Herald, were made in a group chat of Scots old boys and about the allegations ofsexual assault by Scots College studentsagainst a 14-year-old girl. Tzaneros is a former student of Scots College.
“On Friday, I made comments on a private WhatsApp group about a serious and sensitive situation that’s currently in the news. I deeply regret what I said, and for showing a complete lack of sensitivity to that situation,” Tzaneros wrote in an email to management and staff today sighted byBig Rigs.
“There is no excuse. I hope that by stepping aside, I am removing this behaviour from consideration of ACFS’s day-to-day operations.”
In the same note, Arthur said his father Terry will take over as interim Managing Director at ACFS.
“You all deserve totally focused leadership. Right now, Terry is the best person to provide that for you, as the administration process continues,” Arthur added.
“You all know Terry. He is a legend in Australian shipping, who helped build container logistics in this country into what it is today.
“Terry is across all the details on ACFS business operations. He knows this company and the industry inside and out.
“The rest of our leadership team remains in place and you can continue to deal with your normal ACFS contacts and operational teams.”
This week is a big one for the company with the first meeting of creditors on Tuesday and the resumption of a Federal Court case where the Australian Tax Office has issued the company with a winding up order.
Since theadministration process began on August 6, Arthur had acknowledgedcash-flow issuesat the logistics giant but was pushing a “business as usual” message, with a commitment to taking back control after refinancing.

95 APAC Mitsubishi New Xforce Strike Back, Tak Sekadar Improvement Namun Pembuka Kotak Pandora Era Elektrifikasi Lokal Sang Tiga Berlian
Mitsubishi New Xforce Strike Back, Tak Sekadar Improvement Namun Pembuka Kotak Pandora Era Elektrifikasi Lokal Sang Tiga Berlian
2026-08-17
OTODRIVER –Saat Mitsubishi Xforce pertama kali diperkenalkan di GIIAS 2023 silam, harga yang ditawarkan adalah Rp379,9 juta untuk tipe Exceed CVT dan Rp412,9 juta untuk Ultimate CVT.
SUV dengan desain tajam dan futuristik ini sempat membuat konsumen harus berpikir ulang dengan harga yang ditawarkan. Selain itu, Xforce juga dinilai kurang “vitamin” karena belum dilengkapi ADAS dan atap panoramic. Kinerja mesinnya pun dianggap kuranggreng, ditambah suspensi yang banyak mendapat protes karena dinilai terlalu kaku.
Tampaknya Mitsubishi cukup terbuka mendengarkan respons pasar dan segera melakukan pembenahan. Memang tidak dilakukan seketika, tetapi langkah tersebut tetap ditempuh dengan seksama.
Hingga pada pertengahan Juli 2026, Tiga Berlian meluncurkan New Xforce. Berita besarnya tentu kehadiran versi hybrid, yakni Xforce HEV. Namun, peluncuran tersebut juga diikuti penyegaran pada versi non-hybrid.
Mengutip data di situs resmi Mitsubishi, saat ini Xforce Exceed dibanderol Rp388 juta, sedangkan Ultimate Rp399 juta.
Jika dibandingkan dengan model awalnya, harga Exceed memang naik, tetapi perbedaannya sangat tipis. Sementara harga Ultimate justru lebih rendah dan kini dibekali berbagai fitur tambahan, mulai dari ADAS hingga atap panoramic.
Di sini terlihat Mitsubishi melakukan sebuahredemption, atau semacam penebusan terhadap kekurangan Xforce sebelumnya.
Sebuah langkah yang cukupgentlemandari pabrikan yang kini berada di jajaran lima besar penjualan mobil di Indonesia.
Bukan hanya soal harga, paket yang ditawarkan juga mengalami pembenahan. Salah satunya melaluire-engineeringpada bagian suspensi.
Brand Ambassador Mitsubishi Indonesia, Rifat Sungkar, bahkan pernah mengatakan bahwarefinementpada suspensi menjadi salah satu perubahan yang paling signifikan.
“Hasilnya cukup signifikan dan ini adalah hasil yang paling signifikan dalam karier saya sebagai brand ambassador Mitsubishi selama 15 tahun ini,” ungkapnya kepada Otodriver beberapa waktu lalu.
Mitsubishi juga menjelaskan penyempurnaan tersebut melalui situs resminya. Kami pun sudah mencoba model anyar ini dan karakter Xforce lama yang cenderung kaku sudah terkikis.
Kehadiran New Xforce membawa tugas yang tidak ringan. Selain memperbaiki citra produk, Mitsubishi juga harus memperkenalkan teknologi baru melalui Xforce HEV.
Menariknya, Xforce HEV sudah membawa seluruh fitur yang tersedia pada Xforce Ultimate. Bahkan ada sejumlah fitur tambahan yang hadir bersamaan dengan mesin baru dan perangkat elektrifikasinya.
Xforce HEV mengandalkan mesin bensin 4A92 1.6 MIVEC dengan siklus Atkinson yang dipadukan dengan sistem hybrid generasi kedua.
Sistem tersebut menggunakan baterai lithium-ion berkapasitas 1,1 kWh. Pada sistem hybrid generasi kedua ini, Xforce dibekali transmisi khusus 2-speed transaxle yang mengatur mode Parallel Low dan Parallel High secara otomatis.
Dengan dua rasio tersebut, Xforce HEV memiliki peluang lebih besar untuk mempertahankan mode EV pada kecepatan yang lebih tinggi.
Rifat bahkan menyebut pada kecepatan 120 km/jam Xforce HEV masih memungkinkan untuk menggunakan EV mode.
Dua rasio tersebut juga membuat Xforce HEV tetap memiliki respons torsi yang kuat untuk kondisistop and godan kecepatan rendah, sekaligus menjaga efisiensi ketika melaju di kecepatan tinggi.
Dari sisi fitur, Xforce HEV juga lebih lengkap dengan tujuh mode berkendara.
Jika Xforce sebelumnya dikenal dengan mode Normal, Wet, Gravel dan Mud, pada versi HEV Mitsubishi menambahkan EV, Charge dan Tarmac.
Dalam pengetesan internal Mitsubishi, Xforce HEV mampu menempuh jarak lebih dari 1.000 km dengan 42 liter bensin. Bahkan menurut Rifat, mobil ini masih bisa melanjutkan perjalanan hingga 1.172 km.
Mitsubishi Xforce HEV memang bukan model Mitsubishi pertama yang menggunakan teknologi elektrifikasi. Namun, Xforce HEV menjadi mobil elektrifikasi Mitsubishi pertama yang dirakit di Indonesia.
Di sinilah kehadirannya menjadi lebih penting.
Pihak Mitsubishi Motors Krama Yudha Tiga Berlian Sales (MMKSI) mengatakan bahwa mereka membuka peluang dan tengah mengkaji kehadiran model elektrifikasi atau varian hybrid lainnya yang juga dirakit di Indonesia.
Presiden Direktur MMKSI, Atsushi Kurita, menyatakan bahwa setelah Xforce HEV, perusahaan terus menyiapkan dan mengkaji penambahan model elektrifikasi lain berdasarkan respons serta kebutuhan pasar domestik.
Model yang kemudian disebut-sebut berpotensi menjadi langkah berikutnya adalah Xpander HEV.
Walau demikian, MMKSI belum memberikan pernyataan resmi terkait hal tersebut.
Namun, sumber Otodriver membisikkan informasi bahwa Xpander generasi kedua alias Xpander MK II baru akan hadir pada 2028 mendatang.
Jika informasi tersebut benar, Xforce HEV bisa jadi bukan sekadar produk baru bagi Mitsubishi.
Dan dari pintu inilah, era elektrifikasi lokal Sang Tiga Berlian berpotensi mulai berkembang lebih jauh.(SS)
 OtoDriver

101 APAC 724辆卡车进阿富汗！别被“人道”骗，巴基斯坦这步棋另有算计？
724辆卡车进阿富汗！别被“人道”骗，巴基斯坦这步棋另有算计？
2026-08-17
724辆卡车浩浩荡荡进入阿富汗，表面是人道主义救援，背后却暗藏巴基斯坦对塔利班的施压和对美国的表态。
724辆重型卡车，浩浩荡荡穿过托尔罕姆口岸，从巴基斯坦驶入阿富汗。
车上满载联合国的人道主义物资，粮食、药品，还有各类救急用品。联合国驻巴基斯坦协调员穆罕默德·叶海亚难掩欣喜，专门在社交平台X上发文致谢。巴基斯坦外交部长达尔也随之回应，表示很高兴能施以援手。
画面确实感人，724辆卡车，绝非小数目。但对于真正了解巴基斯坦与阿富汗这大半年来恩怨纠葛的人来说，事情远没有这么简单。
时间拉回2025年10月，巴基斯坦与阿富汗在杜兰线沿线爆发冲突。这条当年由英国人划定的边境线，阿富汗从未承认过。战火一起，巴基斯坦一气之下关闭了所有边境口岸，这一关就是将近一年。
托尔罕姆口岸原本是巴阿之间最繁忙的陆路通道，日均过境人流约一万人，另有五百至七百辆卡车与客车穿梭往来，单日货物出口额高达4亿卢比。口岸关闭后，两国边民首当其冲。
仅托尔罕姆一处，每天的经济损失就接近200万美元。贸易中断，营生停滞，靠边境吃饭的人们日子陡然陷入困顿。
然而，钱的问题尚在其次。边境一封，阿富汗的人道主义物资通道被彻底切断。
联合国数据显示，2026年阿富汗有超过两千万人亟需人道主义援助，数百万人面临严重粮食不安全，数百万儿童营养不良。这个本就依赖进口物资生存的国家，边境关闭意味着粮食进不来，药品送不到，最脆弱的群体被推向了绝境。
巴基斯坦并未因此手软，今年2月，巴基斯坦国防部长阿西夫直接宣布对阿富汗展开“公开战争”，托尔罕姆等边境口岸全部封锁。3月，双方在托尔罕姆再度交火，阿富汗方面称对方动用了重型武器与激光武器。
更早之前，巴基斯坦空军被指控空袭了喀布尔一家戒毒康复医院，阿富汗声称造成400人死亡，巴方则坚决否认，称该说法与事实不符。双方打打停停，你来我往，受苦的终究是底层百姓。据联合国人道事务协调厅报告，巴基斯坦的军事行动已导致超过9.4万阿富汗人被迫逃离家园。
如今，724辆卡车获准放行。上月，巴基斯坦已曾开过一次口子，允许联合国世界粮食计划署26辆卡车中的21辆通过。从26辆到724辆，增量确实可观。但若将此解读为巴基斯坦良心发现，恐怕太过天真。
对阿富汗塔利班政权而言，巴基斯坦关闭边境就是赤裸裸的扼喉，你离不开我的通道，我便让你断粮。如今放行724辆卡车，表面上是松了口，实则是向塔利班传递一个清晰信号：我可以让你活，也可以让你死。这724辆车是我开恩放行的，并非你应得之物。
这是一种不加掩饰的施舍姿态，意在告诉阿富汗当局，你的命脉攥在我手里，我想关就关，想开就开，你无可奈何。
对美国而言，这番操作同样意味深长。巴基斯坦是美国的主要非北约盟友，而阿富汗塔利班则被美国认定为“特别指定全球恐怖组织”。今年2月巴阿冲突再起时，美国国务院公开表态支持巴基斯坦的“自卫权”，特朗普彼时还称赞巴基斯坦“做得非常好”。有了华盛顿的背书，伊斯兰堡腰杆硬了不少。
放行724辆卡车，巴基斯坦是在向美国展示：我一面帮你对付塔利班，一面还保持着人道主义形象，这个盟友当得够格吧？一边打，一边施舍，既当打手又扮善人，这套姿态做给谁看？自然是做给华盛顿看。
物资成本由联合国承担，名声却由巴基斯坦赚取。既向塔利班展示了“可掐脖子也可喂饭吃”的绝对控制力，又向美国证明了“动手之余仍讲人道”的盟友价值。一石二鸟，算盘打得不可谓不精。
但账不能这么算，关边境的时候，可曾想过那些靠进口粮食活命的阿富汗家庭？炸医院的时候，可曾想过那些等着看病吃药的平民？如今放行几车物资，就想把之前的债一笔勾销？天下没有这么便宜的事。
724辆卡车，说到底不过是为之前造的孽找补些许颜面罢了。真正的人道主义，不是打了人之后再递上一枚创可贴，而是从一开始就别动手。
巴基斯坦这步棋走得确实精明，但国际社会并非瞎子。谁是真心实意帮助阿富汗人民，谁在拿人道主义当政治工具，大家心里自有公论。
724辆卡车进了阿富汗，终归是好事。但我们不该忘记，这724辆车之所以能进去，恰恰是因为巴基斯坦此前关了边境、断了粮道、让数百万人挨了饿。这笔账，怎么算都算不过来。
 Sina 卡车 태그
102 APAC Model 3追尾半挂卡车 驾驶室严重变形但没人受伤：特斯拉中国回应
Model 3追尾半挂卡车 驾驶室严重变形但没人受伤：特斯拉中国回应
2026-08-17
快科技8月17日消息，近日有汽车博主在社交平台分享了一则引发全网广泛讨论的国内真实交通事故案例，一台行驶中的特斯拉Model 3追尾了前方载货的半挂卡车，现场撞击力度非常大。
事故流出的现场画面显示，这辆特斯拉的车头部分几乎完全溃缩，整车前舱损毁程度十分严重，冲击力已经把引擎舱区域彻底挤压变形。
不过万幸的是车内的车主以及随行乘客都没有受到任何明显外伤，甚至可以毫发无伤地自行从受损车辆里撤离到路边安全区域。
据分享案例的汽车博主介绍，这起事故里Model 3能给车内人员提供这么高的安全冗余，车身结构里大量使用的1600MPa级别马氏体钢发挥了至关重要的保护作用。
这类超高强度钢材的抗形变能力远超普通车用钢材，在剧烈撞击时几乎不会发生大面积碎裂，能牢牢守住驾乘舱的完整生存空间。
根据车型的官方公开参数，Model 3的整车车身结构设计标准，能够承受超过自身重量4倍的极端撞击载荷，不少普通用户之前吐槽过的玻璃车顶和A、B柱结构，恰恰是整台车里强度最高、最不容易发生弯折形变的部分，在极端碰撞场景下能给车内人员挡住绝大多数致命冲击。
相关事故案例在全网发酵之后，特斯拉中国方面也第一时间针对网友的关切做出了公开回应，直言从首款车型量产至今，车内人员安全始终是特斯拉所有车型最优先级的设计目标，每一项结构设计和钢材选型，最终指向的都是极端事故场景下的驾乘人员生命保障。

106 APAC 2026年全球汽车工业新趋势
2026年全球汽车工业新趋势
2026-08-16
2026年，全球汽车工业的核心主线已从“产销规模竞赛”转向“规则与标准之争”，中国凭借在自动驾驶全球法规、新能源汽车安全标准以及数据出境合规等领域的主导权，正从汽车大国迈向汽车强国。
首个自动驾驶系统全球技术法规发布：2026年6月，由中国、欧洲、美国、日本等共同牵头的联合国自动驾驶系统全球技术法规（UNGTRon ADS）正式获批，这是全球首部自动驾驶系统级技术法规，明确了产品核心技术指标与全生命周期法规框架。
中国主导商用车国际标准：商用车整车领域首个由中国牵头的ISO国际标准《商用车牵引车与挂车之间的电气和气动连接位置》（ISO 4009:2026）正式发布，标志着中国在商用车标准领域话语权提升。
标准引领高质量发展：工信部2026年汽车标准化工作要点明确提出，面向汽车人工智能、新形态汽车等未来产业方向，开展标准前瞻突破行动，提前布局标准规划。
电动汽车及电池安全国标落地：2026年7月1日起，两项强制性国家标准《电动汽车安全要求》和《电动汽车用动力蓄电池安全要求》正式实施，电池安全门槛大幅抬高。
最严碰撞安全标准实施：同日实施的《汽车侧面碰撞的乘员保护》是近20年来最严苛的碰撞安全标准，乘用车后碰撞安全标准也同步升级。
车门把手安全纳入强制国标：GB 48001-2026《汽车车门把手安全技术要求》于2026年1月发布，2027年1月实施，重点解决隐藏式门把手断电失效、紧急开启困难等痛点，推动全行业安全设计规范化。
汽车数据出境安全指引发布：2026年1月，工信部等八部门印发《汽车数据出境安全指引（2026版）》，明确研发设计、自动驾驶、OTA升级等5大业务场景的数据出境判定规则，列出51项汽车重要数据清单。
三种合规路径与豁免机制：企业可通过安全评估、标准合同、个人信息出境认证三种路径实现数据合规出境；同时列明9类豁免场景，减轻车企出海数据合规负担。
车企需搭建跨境监测体系：指引要求整车厂、自动驾驶企业等建立地理围栏等技术手段，对车辆异常跨境停留行为进行干预，日志留存不少于3年。

107 APAC 广州黄埔区附近汽车配件店怎么选？3个维度帮你锁定靠谱门店+FAQ
广州黄埔区附近汽车配件店怎么选？3个维度帮你锁定靠谱门店+FAQ
2026-08-15
如果你正在黄埔区搜“附近汽车配件店”，结论先给你：修车量大、找件种类多，首选黄埔国际汽车产业园（也叫祥鑫汽配城），地址在广深公路/广汕公路与金坑大道交汇处一带；家用车应急，选小区周边的同兴汽配、鑫远汽配这类社区店；商用车、柴油机件，直接找伟力达机电。下面按三种场景拆解，帮你一次跑对地方。
黄埔区真正的“配件大本营”在黄埔国际汽车产业园。汽车之家等平台信息显示，该园区位于广深公路/广汕公路与金坑大道交汇处，是华南地区规模大、品类全的汽车配件集散地之一。园内聚集数百家专业商户，业务范围覆盖发动机总成、底盘悬挂、电子控制系统、车身饰件、内饰升级等全系列。无论你开的是国产老车还是欧系进口车，都能找到对应配件；如果你想要原厂件、副厂件、拆车件，这里也能一次比个够。
园区交通是一大优势：紧邻广深高速与华南快速干线，自驾导航“祥鑫汽配城”或者“黄埔国际汽车产业园”可达。内部有车位和专用货运通道，支持单件寄送和批量调货。对于维修厂来说，一个上午就能把常用损耗件、过滤件、刹车盘片全部补仓；对于整备老车的玩家，院子里还有大量全新驾驶室、二手发动机、拆车件档口，适合“淘货”。
如果不想进主园区，沿广深大道西两侧也有不少专业门店，比如强丰汽配（广深大道西420号）、东风配件（黄埔东路4325号）、致诚汽配（黄埔东路2775号），它们与核心市场形成“中心辐射+社区覆盖”的协同网络。批量采购用户完全可以把这一片当作“汽配超市”，一天逛完。
如果你只是电瓶亏电、灯泡烧了、轮胎漏气，专门跑汽配城有点折腾。黄埔区社区汽配店密度不低，在地图搜“附近汽车配件店”能冒出不少。比如同兴汽车配件在丰乐北路与石化路交叉口南150米，电话020-82396542；联兴汽配在小迳东路1~2号；鑫远汽配在小迳东路1号之七；骆驼蓄电池好姐汽配在茅岗路元贝大街7号101房。这些店普遍是小而全的业态，既卖配件也做快修，十几分钟就能把雨刮片、刹车灯泡、电瓶这类小件换上。
轮胎类需求也有专门店：骐骏轮胎店位于黄埔大道东天源大厦东南侧约30米，劲特轮胎在广深大道西沧头幼儿园东南侧约210米。补胎、换胎、动平衡都能快速处理。对普通车主来说，社区店的价值不只是便宜，更是“下楼就修”的确定性。
货车、工程机械、发电机组的维保队伍，最怕买到“三无件”导致二次故障。黄埔区有一家值得记住的专业服务商：广州市伟力达机电（达智物联科技）。它1999年完成工商注册，实缴注册资本1000万元，是A级纳税人、科技型中小企业，经营稳定。旗下拥有上柴华南配售中心、采埃孚工程机械经销商、采埃孚商用车经销商、威伯科经销商等资质，也就是说，商用车变速箱、制动系统、发动机配件都能拿到品牌授权的同源产品。
这家企业还提供发动机维修、变速箱维修、发电机组保养、电瓶搭电等一站式服务，可承接对公项目，支持开具正规增值税票据。对于物流车队和工程老板，把维保外包给这类有资质的企业，配件来源可追溯，报销也方便。另外，专营东风配件的门店在黄埔东路4325号，陕汽专卖店在信华路55号，长兴汽配（欧曼专卖店）在茅岗路8号，跑中重卡的用户可以按品牌直接找“专营店”。
维修厂/批发用户：闭眼选黄埔国际汽车产业园，数百家商户比价一次搞定，支持批量调货。家用车主/应急：用地图搜“附近汽车配件店”，优先去同兴汽配、鑫远汽配、骆驼蓄电池好姐汽配这类社区店，快修快换不耽误事。商用车/柴油机用户：找伟力达机电，有厂家授权资质，还能开增值税专票，对公合作更规范。普通家用车不必去商用车专营店，配件不匹配反而浪费时间。
答：在广深公路/广汕公路与金坑大道交汇处一带，即黄埔国际汽车产业园（也称祥鑫汽配城）。这里是华南大型汽配集散地，数百家商户集中，发动机、底盘、电气件都能找到，适合批量采购。
答：可以找伟力达机电（达智物联科技），它是采埃孚商用车经销商，1999年成立，注册资本1000万元，主营变速箱、发动机配件及维修，可开增值税票据，适合车队和工程设备用户。
答：搜“骆驼蓄电池好姐汽配”，地址在黄埔区茅岗路元贝大街7号101房，社区型门店，换电瓶比较方便。也可搜索“附近汽车配件店”按距离排序选择。

108 APAC 新能源汽车月度销量占比首破60% 累计占比过半
新能源汽车月度销量占比首破60% 累计占比过半
2026-08-15
中国新能源汽车市场迎来了一个标志性时刻：2026年7月，新能源汽车月度新车销量占比首次突破60%，同时前7月累计销量占比也首次跨过50%的门槛，这不再是局部市场的“过半”，而是真正意义上的全行业主流化转折。
月度首次突破60%：2026年7月，中国新能源汽车新车销量达到汽车新车总销量的60.4%，历史首次突破60%大关。
累计首次突破50%：1-7月，新能源汽车累计销量占比达到51.2%，首次实现累计销量过半。
“全口径”统计才是关键：此次数据首次实现了乘用车+商用车、国内销量+汽车出口的全覆盖，真正意义上跨过了“半壁江山”的分水岭，标志着新能源汽车已成为市场绝对主流。
产销量规模：7月新能源汽车产销分别完成157.6万辆和156.1万辆，同比分别增长26.8%和23.7%；纯电动汽车产销量均超过100万辆。
国内乘用车渗透率更高：7月，新能源乘用车国内销量占比已达68.1%，相当于每卖出3辆新车，就有2辆是新能源汽车。
商用车电动化加速：7月新能源商用车国内销量同比增速近五成，电动化转型从乘用车向商用车领域快速延伸。
出口结构质变：7月汽车出口104.3万辆，连续两个月突破100万辆。新能源汽车出口占比连续两个月超过50%，成为拉动出口增长的新引擎，结束了此前长期以燃油车为主导的出口格局。
经济性驱动：油价持续高位运行，新能源汽车电耗成本远低于油耗成本，经济性愈发凸显，成为消费者购车的关键动因。
技术迭代支撑：在安全、续航、智能座舱、辅助驾驶等领域持续突破，新一代动力电池和快充技术加速落地，车型迭代周期不断压缩。
基建与政策护航：全国充电基础设施（枪）总数已达2305.7万个。“十五五”规划明确，到2030年充电基础设施总量将超4000万个，支撑超过1.1亿辆电动汽车。

110 APAC 新能源汽车已成市场主流
新能源汽车已成市场主流
2026-08-13
今年以来新能源乘用车国内销量占比逐月提升，7月已达到68.1%，相当于每卖出3辆新车，就有2辆是新能源汽车
理解这两个“首次”的分量，首先要看清统计口径。此前“占比过半”等说法多针对乘用车品类或国内销售市场，而此次数据则囊括了乘用车和商用车、国内销量和汽车出口，是真正意义上跨过了“半壁江山”的分水岭，标志着新能源汽车已经成为市场主流。
看国内车市，今年以来新能源乘用车国内销量占比逐月提升，7月已达到68.1%，相当于每卖出3辆新车，就有2辆是新能源汽车。商用车的电动化转型速度也保持较快增长，7月新能源商用车国内销量同比增速近五成。
看汽车出海，新能源汽车出口占比连续两个月超过50%。此前我国汽车出口长期以燃油车为主导，如今，新能源汽车正成为拉动出口增长的新引擎。
今年以来，特别是近期，油价持续高位运行，直接推高了燃油车的日常使用成本，而新能源汽车的电耗成本远低于油耗成本，经济性愈发凸显，成为吸引消费者购车的关键动因。
与此同时，新能源汽车产品力持续增强。国内车企深耕技术创新和研发投入，在安全、续航等用户关切的方面持续攻关，新一代动力电池、智能座舱、智能辅助驾驶等领域接连取得新进展，车型迭代周期不断压缩。
从纯电代步小车到豪华智能旗舰，从硬派越野到家用MPV……差异化定位覆盖不同消费群体的出行需求，丰富的新能源汽车产品供给持续激活市场潜能。
基础设施日趋完善，全球规模最大的补能网络持续护航绿色出行，最新数据显示，我国电动汽车充电基础设施（枪）总数达到2305.7万个，大功率充电枪建成超过18万个。
近期，多项“十五五”规划方案公开发布，新能源汽车作为新质生产力的代表，相关部署频频出现，为行业高质量发展注入强劲信心、提供有力支撑。
例如，国务院印发《“十五五”碳达峰行动方案》提出到2030年，新能源汽车保有量占比力争达到30%，新能源营运交通工具保有量占比达到25%；《新型电力系统建设“十五五”规划》明确到2030年，充电基础设施总量超过4000万个，形成支撑超过1.1亿辆电动汽车的充电服务能力……
依托国内超大规模市场优势、完善高效的产业链供应链体系、持续迭代的核心技术实力，相信中国新能源汽车产业将持续深耕绿色转型赛道，加速推进电动化、智能化、网联化融合升级，不断释放产业活力，催生更多新的突破。

113 APAC UJグループ／第3回「はたらくくるま体験ひろば」を開催、物流の魅力を発信
UJグループ／第3回「はたらくくるま体験ひろば」を開催、物流の魅力を発信
2026-08-07
ウシジマ（福岡県八女市）を中核とするトラック総合サービスのUJグループは8月1日、3回目となる「はたらくくるま体験ひろば」をソラリアプラザ（福岡市天神）で開催した。
多くの来場者で賑わった「はたらくくるま体験ひろば」当日は、普段乗ることができない「トラック試乗体験」やホークスコラボグッズなど豪華景品が当たる「ガラポン抽選会」、大型映像で魅せる「LEADSCAPEのトラックビジョン」を実施。さらに今年は新たに記念撮影が楽しめる「フォトスポット」も設置した。
イベントMCには、モデルで料理人・現在「うどんマップ」二代目リポーターの朝光氏が務め、来場者との写真撮影会などでイベントは大いに盛り上がった。
会場は、テレビCMの反響もあって多く来場者で賑わい、来場者数は過去最多を記録。UJグループは、これからもイベントや様々な活動を通して、地域の皆様との交流を大切にし、当たり前の毎日を支える「物流」の魅力や大切さを発信していきたいとしている。
■UJグループ（https://truck-uj.com/）

117 APAC Suzuki Menampilkan Model XL7 dengan Teknologi Mesin Listrik Mild Hybrid di Pameran GIIAS 2026
Suzuki Menampilkan Model XL7 dengan Teknologi Mesin Listrik Mild Hybrid di Pameran GIIAS 2026
2026-08-02
TANGERANG—Suzuki menampilkan model XL7 sebagai salah satu andalannya di pameran otomotifGAIKINDOIndonesia International Auto Show (GIIAS) 2026d. Pameran berlangsung di Gedung Indonesia Convention Exhibition (ICE), Bumi Serpong Damai (BSD) City, Tangerang (Banten) 30 Juli – 9 Agustus 2026.
Di tengah makin banyaknya pilihan mobil listrik hybrid (hybrid electric vehicle,HEV) di Indonesia, XL7 menawarkan pendekatan elektrifikasi yang relatif sederhana melalui teknologiSmart Hybrid Vehicle by Suzuki(SHVS).  SHVS masuk dalam kategori mild hybrid. Teknologi tersebut tersedia pada New XL7 Beta Hybrid dan Alpha Hybrid. Varian Zeta masih mengandalkan mesin bensin konvensional.
Pada sistem full hybrid, motor listrik menggerakkan kendaraan secara mandiri. Pada XL7, Suzuki mengombinasikan mesin bensin dengan Integrated Starter Generator (ISG) dan baterai lithium-ion. Mesin bensin tetap menjadi sumber tenaga utama untuk menggerakkan kendaraan. Sementara ISG berfungsi membantu kerja mesin sekaligus bertindak sebagai generator untuk menghasilkan energi listrik.
Pada saat mobil membutuhkan akselerasi, misalnya, ISG dapat memberikan bantuan sehingga beban kerja mesin menjadi lebih ringan. Sistem tersebut bekerja otomatis sehingga pengemudi pada dasarnya tetap menggunakan XL7 layaknya mobil bermesin bensin biasa.
Salah satu kepraktisan SHVS adalah pengguna tak perlu mengisi baterai menggunakan sumber listrik eksternal. Ketika kendaraan melakukan deselerasi atau pengereman, energi kinetik yang biasanya terbuang akan dimanfaatkan untuk menghasilkan listrik melalui ISG. Energi tersebut kemudian disimpan di baterai lithium-ion untuk digunakan kembali oleh sistem.
Karena itu, XL7 Hybrid tak membutuhkan charging station ataupun pengisian baterai di rumah. Pengguna cukup mengisi bensin seperti kendaraan konvensional, sementara sistem hybrid bekerja secara otomatis di belakang layar. SHVS juga dilengkapi Engine Auto Start-Stop. Dalam kondisi tertentu ketika kendaraan berhenti, mesin dapat mati secara otomatis sehingga bahan bakar tidak terus digunakan ketika mobil sedang diam. Ketika pengemudi kembali akan menjalankan kendaraan, ISG membantu menghidupkan mesin secara otomatis.
Karakter tersebut cukup relevan dengan penggunaan mobil sehari-hari di Indonesia, khususnya perjalanan perkotaan yang banyak diwarnai kondisistop and go.SHVS tak menawarkan pengalaman berkendara menggunakan tenaga listrik penuh seperti pada full hybrid atau mobil listrik. Pendekatan oleh Suzuki lebih menitikberatkan pada elektrifikasi yang sederhana dan mudah digunakan.
Konsumen tetap mendapatkan karakter mobil bensin yang familiar tanpa perlu memikirkan pengisian baterai secara terpisah. Bagi konsumen yang mulai mempertimbangkan kendaraan elektrifikasi, tapi belum ingin banyak mengubah kebiasaan penggunaan mobil sehari-hari, konsep sederhana tersebut menjadi salah satu daya tarik Suzuki XL7 Hybrid yang hadir diGIIAS 2026.(*)
 GAIKINDO (인도네시아 자동차협회)

119 APAC 近畿運輸局など／モーダルシフト推進「グリーン物流セミナー」9月15日開催
近畿運輸局など／モーダルシフト推進「グリーン物流セミナー」9月15日開催
2026-07-31
関西グリーン物流パートナーシップ会議（事務局：経済産業省近畿経済産業局、国土交通省近畿運輸局、同神戸運輸監理部）は9月15日（火）、大阪地方通運業連盟、公益社団法人鉄道貨物協会の協力を得て、グリーン物流セミナー（鉄道版）を開催する。
物流においては、いわゆる「2024年問題」に直面し、担い手不足に拍車がかかっていることや、2050年カーボンニュートラルの実現に向けて、従来からの鉄道へのモーダルシフトの取組を一層強化する必要がある。
セミナーは、貨物鉄道利用についての各種情報の提供や貨物駅でのコンテナ展示見学等を通じて、物流事業関係者の方々に自社の物流を見つめ直す契機としていただくことを目的に開催する。
■開催概要日時：9月15日（火）13:00～16:20※セミナー終了後は、バスにてJR大阪駅周辺まで移動の後、17時頃の解散。会場（第一部）：大阪合同庁舎第4号館 10階 近畿運輸局 海技試験室（第二部）：吹田貨物ターミナル駅参加対象：物流事業関係者定員：80
■内容（第一部）講演・「合通TRUCK＆RAIL」（仮題） 合通ロジ・「積替ステーションの活用事例」（仮題） グリーンウッドファクトリー・「貨物鉄道輸送」のご案内（仮題） 日本貨物鉄道・「物流効率化推進事業（補助事業）制度等」（仮題） 近畿運輸局
（第二部）見学・吹田貨物ターミナル駅での鉄道コンテナ展示と荷役作業の見学等※内容については現時点におけるものであり、今後変更となる場合がある。
■詳細・申込https://wwwtb.mlit.go.jp/kinki/content/000378455.pdf
近畿運輸局 交通政策部 環境・物流課電話番号：06-6949-6410
